## ===================== HOW TO EDIT =====================
## Lines starting with ## are comments and are ignored.
## 1) A PAGE starts with:   @@<level> Title | key=value | key=value
##       @@1 = main section on home, @@2 = inside it, @@3 = inside that ... (a page is the child of the nearest
##       previous page with a smaller level).  Keys: code= (fault number), ss= (subsystem no.), icon=, sub=, ch=
## 2) Text under a page is its content. One line = one item:
##       1. step text            numbered step (use real numbers)
##       - bullet text           bullet
##       !h Heading text         bold heading
##       !note Note text         highlighted note box
##       plain text              normal paragraph
##       . text               (start with a backslash if a paragraph must begin with "1." etc.)
## 3) FAULT pages (have code=) use three sections:
##       [SEE]        what the crew sees (message, lamps, effect)
##       [DO]         numbered action steps
##       [ISOLATION]  isolation message / notes
## 4) To add a new page, add a new @@ line.  To reorder, cut/paste whole page blocks (keep levels consistent).
## 5) After editing run:   python txt_to_json.py     then rebuild the app.
## ======================================================


@@1 Fault Troubleshooting | icon=bolt | sub=SS01 – SS19 · find by fault number or message

@@2 SS01  Main Power | ss=1

@@3 F 01 01 P1  VCB stuck in ON position. loco will shut down | code=F 01 01 P1
[SEE]
VCB stuck in ON position. loco will shut down
LSDJ will not glow LSFI flicker and BPFA glows
SR1 and SR2 contactors will open Pantograph will lower, U meter and Bogie meters will come to 0
[DO]
1. Bring throttle to “0” and press BPFA.
2. Try to clear the block section by coasting and bring the train to dead stop.
3. IF LSDJ GLOWS or NOT, do VCU reset ( For Switch OFF/ON CE), after FLG 504 raise Pantograph and close VCB)
4. If unsuccessful, try from rear cab.
5. If un-successful do VCU reset (For Switch OFF/ON CE) and try again.
6. Try to raise pantograph and close VCB and resume traction, if unsuccessful, ask relief loco.
7. Record in log book.
[ISOLATION]
ISOLATION MESSAGE MAIN POWER ISOLATED VCB Inhibited Loco is dead, Press ‘ ENTER ’
LSDJ will not glow LSFI glows
Pantograph will not Raise

@@3 F 01 02 P1  VCB STUCK IN OFF POSITION Try to close VCB again | code=F 01 02 P1
[SEE]
VCB STUCK IN OFF POSITION Try to close VCB again
LSDJ glows, LSFI flickers and BPFA will glow
VCB control changed to redundant processor
[DO]
1. Bring throttle to “0” and press BPFA. After acknowledging wait for 20 sec and try, (VCB pressure to raise)
2. Ensure VCB COC in open position
3. Close VCB. If VCB closed then resume traction.
4. Otherwise, coast to clear the block section (Note: VCD remains in service) if possible and bring the train to dead stop.
5. Do VCU reset (For Switch OFF/ON CE). Raise pantograph, close VCB and resume Traction. If not successful, try from rear cab
6. If all are normal, first isolate bogie-1 by 154-’I’ and do VCU reset (For Switch OFF/ON CE) and try.
7. If unsuccessful, Place 154 on II position and do VCU reset (For Switch OFF/ON CE) and try.
8. Once again do VCU reset (For Switch OFF/ON CE) and try.
9. If unsuccessful ask relief loco and record in logbook
[ISOLATION]
Isolation Message MAIN POWER ISOLATED VCB Inhibited Loco is dead, press “ENTER”
LSDJ and LSFI will glows
VCB will not close
!note NOTE:
While pressing BLDJ, if VCB not close without any fault message and Node remains at 550, Press 136.4 contactor in SB1 Panel.
VCB Gauge

@@3 F 01 03 P1  LOW LSDJ Pantograph | code=F 01 03 P1
[SEE]
LOW LSDJ Pantograph
[DO]
1. Bring throttle to “0” and press BPFA.
2. Press ZPT to DN command and ensure FLG- 504
3. Ensure IG-38 key is horizontal in E70 and vertical in CCB2.
4. Ensure PAN 1 and 2 COC are in horizontal position.
5. Ensure the RS pressure is more than 6 Kg/cm 2 in pneumatic panel.
6. If pressure is low, and MCPA not working, check MCB 48.1 in SB2. If tripped reset the MCB. Check MCPA contactor 48.2 is closed, if not, tap the pressure switch 26, (5 th from left on pneumatic panel in E70 and PANS- PS in CCB2)
PRESSURE glows, will PANTO / LSFI not raise FAULTY flickers U meter PANTO and shows ‘0’ BPFA will glow
UL coc in KNORR pneumatic panel
7. If MCPA works and pressure not building up: In E70 ensure CPA drain cock is closed (parallel to pipe). In CCB-2 ensure PAN-TP or RS drain cock is closed.(back side of pneumatic panel) Close UL cock in CCB2 panel and normalise after building up of MR pressure.(In KNORR loco ensure RS/Pantograph reservoir drain coc in closed condition)
8. If Pantograph is raised, pressure is available and fault message appears, gently tap the pressure switch no.7 th
and 8 th from left (130.4/1 & 130.4/2 available in E70 panel) and PAN1 and PAN2 pressure switch in CCB2 panel.
9. If the above pressure switch is not closed it disables VCB or if VCB closes it will open after 2 seconds
10. Change the position of pantograph selector switch (85) on pneumatic panel and try to raise other pantograph.
11. If both pantograph are not rising from one cab. Switch OFF/ON CE and try. Try by changing the cab Inform to TLC and record in log book

@@3 F 01 04 P1  Catenary Voltage Out Of Limit | code=F 01 04 P1
[SEE]
Catenary Voltage Out Of Limit
LSDJ glows, LSFI flickers and BPFA will glow
VCB opens automatically when catenary voltage is below 17.5KV or above 29 KV
[DO]
1. Bring throttle to “0’’ and stop the train.
2. If BP pressure drops suddenly, switch on flasher light and act as per G&SR. Press BPFA
3. If ‘U’ meter is not deviating, lower pantograph and raise the pantograph.
4. When pantograph touches OHE, a minor spark with ‘choo’ sound indicates tension is available. Confirm with TLC/TPC
5. If there is no OHE supply, wait for catenary voltage to raise above17.5 KV but less than 30 KV.
6. Ensure OHE voltage in simulation screen, if it is above 29KV inform to TPC/TLC to reduce voltage. After OHE voltage restored, reclose the VCB and resume traction.
7. If OHE is available and U meter not deviating, lower Pantograph and check 2A PT fuse in SB1 panel, renew with spare fuse.
8. If U meter deviates, check DDS messages. If DDS Message is SLG1-0020/VIU1-0020/CCUO-0043 isolate SR-1 by 154 in1 (SB1). If the DDS message is SLG2-0020/VIU2-0020/CCUO: 0044 isolate SR-2 by 154 in II position and try.
9. If the fault message from both SLG1 0020/ VIU1-0020/CCUO- 0043 and SLG2 0020/VIU2-0020/CCUO-0044, ensure OHE supply is normal, if NO tension indication prevails in loco, change the pantograph and try. If unsuccessful do VCU reset (For Switch OFF/ON CE) and try.
10. If the bogie is isolated automatically on run or while closing VCB, work with single bogie and do VCU reset (For Switch OFF/ON CE) at convenient place and try to bring both bogie to service.
11. Try by changing pantograph and cab. If Unsuccessful, contact TLC and record in log book.

@@3 F 01 05 P1  Transformer Oil Temp Or Pressure Not Ok | code=F 01 05 P1
[SEE]
Transformer Oil Temp Or Pressure Not Ok
TE/BE reduction or VCB trips Try to close VCB if open
LSDJ may glow, LSFI flickers BPFA glows
Possibility of reduced TE/BE. GTO pulsing will stop.
Hotel load will off and VCB will trip.
[DO]
1. If TE is available then clear the block section by traction. If VCB trips try to clear section by coasting.
2. Bring throttle to “0” and press BPFA.
3. Check the oil level in both the expansion tanks of transformer and converters in machine room. It should be above minimum mark.
4. Check both transformer oil pumps and OCB MCB 62.1/1,59.1/1 (HB-1) and 62.1/2, 59.1/2 (HB-2)
5. If any MCB tripped open VCB and rest once.
6. If any MCB is repeatedly tripping or oil pumps/OCB are not working, keep corresponding MCB in open condition and isolate concerned SR by changing 154 to I or II position after opening VCB.
7. If LP noticed that OCB impeller and its casing physically damaged, then isolate the damaged OCB and its concerned pump by opening its MCB.
8. If MCB are normal, ensure the working of transformer oil pumps and OCB. Keep loco in idle mode for 10 min.
9. If Main power is isolated, Do VCU reset (For Switch OFF/ON CE) and try.
10. If unable to energize, check DDS messages related to “Transformer temperature>Limit”
- If DDS message is SLG1:0019/VIU1:0019 isolate SR1 (154 to I and open 127.1/1)
- If fault message is SLG2:0019/VIU2:0019 isolate SR2 (154 to II and open127.1/2) Switch OFF/ ON CE and try.
- For BTIL make SR, to isolate Bogie Switch 154-1 or 2 as trial and error.
- In BTIL SR, if transformer temp is high fault is there, then only popup message will be there. LP has to acknowledge and work further.
[ISOLATION]
Isolation Message SS01 : Main Power MAIN POWER ISOLATED Press ENTER
LSDJ & LSFI glows
VCB trips. VCB inhibited Loco is dead
11. If DDS message is FLG1 0086/ICP1:0086 (Dis. In both Transformer oil circuits- pressure related) or transformer oil pumps MCB repeatedly tripping, isolate BUR-2. Do VCU reset (For Switch OFF/ON CE) and try.
12. If unsuccessful, ask relief loco and record in log Book.

@@3 F 01 06 P1  Filter ON/OFF Contactor Struck On. VCB will not close again | code=F 01 06 P1
[SEE]
Filter ON/OFF Contactor Struck On. VCB will not close again
LSDJ glows, LSFI flickers& BPFA will glow
Filter discharging contactor will open
[DO]
1. Bring throttle to “0” and press BPFA
2. Try to clear the block section by coasting and bring the train to dead stop.
3. Check machine room, Filter block for any smoke, smell or any abnormality.
4. If there is NO abnormality Do VCU reset (For Switch OFF/ON CE) and try.
5. Raise pantograph and close VCB and resume traction if loco is normal.
6. Check 127.1/1 or 127.1/2 MCB is tripped in SB panel in ABB prop locos
[ISOLATION]
Isolation Message SS01 : Main Power MAIN POWER ISOLATED Press ENTER
LSDJ & LSFI Will glows
VCB trips VCB inhibited Loco is dead
7. If DDS message is SLG1-0059/VIU1:0059 / VCI: 0032 /CCUO: 0176- Contactor 8.1 stuck on fatal, isolate SR-1 by open 127.1/1 and 154-1 in SB1.
8. Do VCU reset (For Switch OFF/ON CE) and try.
9. If unsuccessful or any abnormality ask relief loco and record in log book

@@3 F 01 07 P1  Pre-charge Or Main Contactor Stuck ON | code=F 01 07 P1
[SEE]
Pre-charge Or Main Contactor Stuck ON
LSDJ & BPFA glows, LSFI flicker
VCB trips VCB will not close again
[DO]
1. Bring throttle to “0” and press BPFA.
2. Try to clear the block section by coasting and bring the train to dead stop. Check machine room (SR1 and SR2)for any smoke, smell or any abnormality
3. Do VCU reset (For Switch OFF/ON CE)
[ISOLATION]
Isolation Message SS01 : Main Power MAIN POWER ISOLATED Press ENTER
LSDJ & LSFI glows
VCB trips VCB inhibited Loco is dead
4. If unsuccessful, check the back ground message, a. SLG1 0037 or 0039 isolate SR1 by opening 127.1/1 in SB1 Panel and 154-1 Position in SB1. b. SLG2-0037 or 0039 isolate SR-2 by opening 127.1/2 in SB2 Panel and 154-II position in SB2. c. In MEDHA & ABB if main power gets isolated at 504 node itself ask for relief d. (Medha and ABB SR Bogie isolation will not be succeeded)
5. Do VCU reset (For Switch OFF/ON CE) and try.
6. Work the train with single bogie if load and road permits then talk to TLC and act accordingly.
7. If unsuccessful o r any abnormalities ask relief loco and record in log book

@@3 F 01 08 P1  PRIMARY OVER CURRENT Check OCR 78 target drops Close VCB after RESET relay | code=F 01 08 P1
[SEE]
PRIMARY OVER CURRENT Check OCR 78 target drops Close VCB after RESET relay
LSDJ glows, LSFI flickers& BPFA glows
VCB trips 1. Do not close VCB until you check the loco particularly OCR 78. Check for any fire in machine rooms. If fire is noticed act according to fault message F1501P1 or any abnormality ask for relief loco.
2. If no fire and no abnormality, coast to clear block section. Bring throttle to 0 and stop the train.
3. Check OCR-78 flag in SB-1 panel.
4. Check the oil level in both the expansion tanks of transformer and converters. Check for any oil splash through vent pipe of transformer. If there is any abnormality like splashing of oil inside the machine room or from transformer/converter, sign of overheating /sparking of connection; shut down and ask for relief loco
5. Inspect the machine room for any oil spillage.
Isolation Message Main Power Isolated VCB inhibited Loco is dead
LSDJ & LSFI Glows
VCB will not close 6. If OCR 78 is tripped and there is no abnormality of oil splashing and oil level is normal in all the four gauges, then reset the relay. To reset turn the reset knob clockwise and ensure trip indication disappears. Press BPFA.(Close VCB only after resetting OCR78)
7. Close VCB and inform TLC and record in the logbook.
8. If not successful after making one attempt as given above then VCB will be inhibited, ask for relief loco.
9. If already one closing attempt is made ask for relief loco. Record in log book.

@@3 F 01 09 P1  Aux. Winding Over Current | code=F 01 09 P1
[SEE]
Aux. Winding Over Current
LSDJ & BPFA glows, LSFI flickers
DJ trips 1. Bring throttle to “0” and press BPFA. Close VCB and resume traction.
2. If problem persists isolation message will appear, acknowledge by pressing enter button.
3. Do VCU reset (For Switch OFF/ON CE) Check the back ground message and isolate BUR1 or BUR2 or BUR3 one by one by switching OFF 127.22/1 (SB-1) or 127.22/2 (SB-2) or 127.22/3 (SB-2) and try. Before normalizing MCB, switch OFF CE, reset MCB, and switch ON CE.
4. If unsuccessful ask relief loco and record in log book.
Isolation Message Main Power Isolated VCB inhibited Loco is dead
LSDJ & LSFI Glows
VCB will not close

@@3 F 01 10 P1  Fatal Error In Main Circuit | code=F 01 10 P1
[SEE]
Fatal Error In Main Circuit
Turn off the Loco
LSDJ glows, LSFI Flickers &BPFA Glows
VCB will open and Pantograph will lower U meter will show ‘0’
[DO]
1. Bring throttle to “0” and press BPFA.
2. Try to clear the block section by coasting and bring the train to dead stop.
3. Ensure 154 switch is normal.
4. Check all MCB in SB1 or 2 panels, if any MCB is tripped, reset the same after switching off CE and try.
[ISOLATION]
Isolation Message Main Power Isolated VCB inhibited Loco is dead
LSDJ & LSFI Glows
5. In some IGBT BUR locos 3phase MRB and TMSB connected to BUR2.
6. If any MCB tripped in HB1 or 2 panels reset once after opening VCB.
7. If any MCB repeatedly tripping or any abnormality is noticed in related auxiliary motor, do not reset the MCB and isolate its related bogie. Do VCU reset (For Switch OFF/ON CE) and try to bring back Main power into service.
8. Check the back ground message. If fault message is Life sign missing from FLG1 or 2, STB1or 2, HBB 1or 2 or no fault message, Do VCU reset (For Switch OFF/ON CE)
8. If not successful Do VCU reset (For Switch OFF/ON CE). Raise pantograph and close VCB and resume traction.
9. If more than one BUR is isolated. Troubleshoot as per additional instructions given below for SS01 (OCB, TMB, SR pump,TFR pump, MCP and CHBA).
10. If unsuccessful and VCB cannot be closed ask for relief loco
Additional Instructions for SS01 (See DDS and act accordingly)
1. Isolation of Auxiliary Converter-1 and then Auxiliary Converter-2 (Fault in OCB 1 or 2)
If there is earth fault in OCB-1 or OCB-2 with following fault messages. a) STB2:004: Earth fault in Auxiliary circuit b) Auxiliary converter -1 isolated due to inverter over current or inverter fault c) Auxiliary converter-2 isolated due to
If there is no tripping of MCB 59.1/1 or 59.1/2, Do VCU reset (For Switch OFF/ON CE)
1. Isolate OCB-1by open MCB 59.1/1 (HB-1) and close VCB. If no fatal error message isolate bogie-1 by keeping 154 in 1 position. Half TE/BE will be available.
2. If again BUR-1 and then BUR-2 failed, normalise 59.1/1 and isolate OCB-2 by open MCB 59.1/2 (HB-2)
inverter over current or inverter fault d) Main power off. VCB inhibited 3) Do VCU reset (For Switch OFF/ON CE) and close VCB. If no abnormalities isolate bogie-2 by keeping 154 in 2 position. Half TE/BE will be available.
2. Isolation of Auxiliary Converter-2 and then Auxiliary Converter-1(Fault in TMB 1or 2)
If there is earth fault in TMB-1 or TMB-2 with following fault messages.
a. STB2:004: Earth fault in Auxiliary circuit
b. Auxiliary converter -2 isolated due to inverter over current or inverter fault
c. Auxiliary converter-1 isolated due to inverter over current. or inverter fault
d. Main power off. VCB inhibited
If there is no tripping of MCB 53.1/1 or 53.1/2. Do VCU reset (For Switch OFF/ON CE) Some locos TMSCB 1and 2 and MRB 1 and 2 connected to BUR2. In that case isolate the same and try.
1. Isolate TMB-1 by open MCB 53.1/1 (HB-1) and close VCB. If no fatal error message isolate bogie-1 by keeping 154 in 1 position. Half traction will be available.
2. If again BUR-2 and then BUR-1 failed, normalise 53.1/1 and isolate TMB-2 by open MCB 53.1/2 (HB-2).
3. Do VCU reset (For Switch OFF/ON CE) and close VCB. If no fatal error message isolate bogie-2 by keeping 154 in 2 position. Half TE/BE will be available.
3.Isolation Of Auxiliary Converter- 2 And Then Auxiliary Converter-3 (Fault In Converter Oil Pump And/or Transformer Oil Pump)
If there is earth fault in aux converter or transformer oil pump with following fault messages.
a. STB2:004: Earth fault in Auxiliary circuit
b. Auxiliary converter -2 isolated due to inverter over current or inverter fault
If there is no tripping of MCB 62.1/1 or 62.1/2 or 63.1/1 or 63.1/2 isolate defective auxiliary motor using the following procedure. Do VCU reset (For Switch OFF/ON CE)
1. Isolate SR Oil Pump motor-1 by open MCB 63.1/1(HB-1).Close VCB. If no fatal error message isolate bogie-1 by keeping 154 in 1 position. Half TE/BE will be available.
c. Auxiliary converter-3 isolated due to inverter over current. or inverter fault
d. Main power off. VCB inhibited
2. If again BUR-2 and then BUR-3 failed, normalise 63.1/1 and isolate SR Oil Pump motor-2 by open MCB 63.1/2 (HB-2).
3. Do VCU reset (For Switch OFF/ON CE) Close VCB. If no fatal error message isolate bogie-2 by keeping 154 in 2 position. Half TE/BE will be available.
4. If again BUR-2 and then BUR-3 failed, normalise 63.1/2 and isolate TFP Oil Pump motor-1 by open MCB 62.1/1 (HB-1).
5. Close VCB. If no fatal error message, resume traction. 70% of the TE/BE available
6. If again BUR-2 and then BUR-3 failed, normalise 62.1/1 and isolate TFP OP motor -2 by open MCB 62.1/2 (HB-2).
7. Do VCU reset (For Switch OFF/ON CE), Close VCB. If no fatal error message, resume traction.70% of the TE/BE available.
4. Isolation of Auxiliary Converter-3 and then Auxiliary Converter-2 (Fault in MCP/Battery charger)
If there is earth fault in MCP-1 or MCP-2 with following fault messages.
a. STB2:004: Earth fault in Auxiliary circuit b. Auxiliary converter -3 isolated due to inverter over current or inverter fault c. Auxiliary converter-2 isolated due to inverter over current. or inverter fault d. Main power off. VCB inhibited
1. If there is no tripping of MCB 47.1/1 or 47.1/2 isolates defective MCP by following instructions. Do VCU reset (For Switch OFF/ON CE).
2. Isolate MCP1 by open MCB 47.1/1 (HB-1)
3. Close VCB. If no abnormality work with MCP-2
4. If again BUR-3 and then BUR-2 failed, normalise 47.1/1 and isolate MCP-2 by open 47.1/2. Do VCU reset (For Switch OFF/ON CE), close VCB and work with MCP-1
5. Switch OFF 100 MCB and try.
6. In ABB IGBT BUR Switch OFF 110 MCB(since MCB 100 not available)
5. Isolation of Auxiliary Converter-3 and then Auxiliary Converter-1 (Fault in Scavenging Blower)
If there is earth fault in TM SCB-1 or TM SCB-2 with following fault messages. a) STB2:004: Earth fault in Auxiliary circuit b) Auxiliary converter -3 isolated due to inverter over current or inverter fault c) Auxiliary converter-1 isolated due to inverter over current. or inverter fault d) Main power off. VCB inhibited
If there is no tripping of MCB No.55.1/1 or 55.1/2 Isolate both TM scavenging blower and work up to destination.
If there is no tripping of MCB No.55.1/1 or 55.1/2 Isolate both TM scavenging blower and work up to destination
!note Note: Main power isolated due to SR1 or 2 fault messages
1. If the back ground messages are related to SR1or 2, isolate concern SR by opening 127.1/1 for SR1 and 127.1/2 for SR2. Do VCU reset (For Switch OFF/ON CE) and try. If unsuccessful ask relief loco and record in log book.
2. If all MCB in HB1/2 panels are normal or if 127.24 tripped in SB2 with “Converter Coolant pressure not ok” and both 63.1/1 and 63.1/2 alternatively tripping - isolate BUR2, switch OFF CE, reset MCB 127.24, switch ON CE and try.
3. If all MCB in HB1/2 panels are normal with “Converter oil temperature too high” or both 59.1/1 and 59.1/2 alternatively tripping
- isolate BUR1
4. In case of MU Locos, if leading loco fails, place 154 on I + II position and clear section with leading loco till the battery voltage reaches 92 volts

@@3 F 01 01 P2  Over | code=F 01 01 P2
[SEE]
Over
Temperature
Control
Electronics
LSCE & BPFA Glows
Do not switch off CE, first clear section. Throttle will respond normally (If CE is switched off when LSCE is glowing, it is not possible to switch on CE again. Wait for LSCE to extinguish in cooling mode)
[DO]
1. Press BPFA.
2. Check MRB MCB 54.1/1 and 54.1/2 (HB 1and 2) and 127.91/1 and 127.91/2 in SB panels. If any MCB tripped, reset once after opening VCB and try.
3. If MCBs are normal, clear section, bring throttle to 0 and secure the train.
4. Inform SM and TLC, regarding cooling operation. A-9 to be kept in emergency.
5. Switch OFF the CE. Put BL key in position C and raise pantograph and close VCB. Ensure both MRBs are working. Wait for LSCE to extinguish.
6. If VCB trips during cooling mode check OCR 78. If OCR78 tripped follow troubleshooting F 01 08 P1.
7. After LSCE extinguishes, open VCB, lower pantograph and switch ON CE. Raise pantograph and close VCB. Inform TLC and record in log book.

@@3 F 01 02 P2  TRANSFORMER OIL PRESSURE NOT OK. Any oil pump Circuit not Working TE/BE will be… | code=F 01 02 P2
[SEE]
TRANSFORMER OIL PRESSURE NOT OK. Any oil pump Circuit not Working TE/BE will be Reduced
BPFA will glow Possibility of reduced TE/BE. VCB will open. Hotel load will not work
[DO]
1. Press BPFA. 70% TE/BE available.
2. Ensure that transformer oil Pump 1 and 2 working by touching the pumps outlets and inlet pipes in the under frame, any oil leakages and its MCB are normal. If normal work on wards (Check oil level in expansion tanks. If level decrease, trip VCB, STOP train, and check for oil leakage.)
3. If 62.1/1 in HB1 or 62.1/2 in HB2 panels tripped, trip VCB, reset MCB and try.
4. If again tripped do not reset and if load and road permits, work with 70% TE/BE.
5. Inform TLC and record in the loco log book.

@@3 F 01 03 P2  EARTH FAULT AUX. WINDING CIRCUIT | code=F 01 03 P2
[SEE]
EARTH FAULT AUX. WINDING CIRCUIT
BPFA will glow
[DO]
1. Press BPFA.
2. Check machine room for burning smell of auxiliaries. If any abnormality trip MCB of that auxiliary and act accordingly.
3. Inform TLC and make a remark in the logbook.

@@3 F 01 04 P2  LOW FREQUENCY CATENARY VOLTAGE | code=F 01 04 P2
[SEE]
LOW FREQUENCY CATENARY VOLTAGE
BPFA will glow Traction will start above 45Hz
[DO]
1. Press BPFA. Bring throttle to zero and wait for 1 min and set TE/BE again.
2. Keep trying for traction and resume normal operation,
3. Inform TLC at scheduled stop and record in the logbook.

@@3 F 01 05 P2  CATENARY FREQUENCY IS HIGH | code=F 01 05 P2
[SEE]
CATENARY FREQUENCY IS HIGH
BPFA will glow Traction will start less than 55Hz.
[DO]
1. Press BPFA. Bring throttle to zero
2. Keep trying for traction and resume normal operation.
3. Inform TLC at scheduled stop and record in the logbook.

@@3 F 01 06 P2  Main Power AUXILIARY CAPACITOR OF MRB NOT OFF | code=F 01 06 P2
[SEE]
Main Power AUXILIARY CAPACITOR OF MRB NOT OFF
BPFA will glow Nil Continue normal traction
[DO]
1. Press BPFA.
2. If machine room blower 1 and 2 working, continue normal working.
3. Inform TLC at scheduled stop and make a remark in the logbook

@@3 F 01 07 P2  Demanded Speed Can Not Be Achieved | code=F 01 07 P2
[SEE]
Demanded Speed Can Not Be Achieved
LSP may glow Unable to haul or While starting, if experience speed not increasing more than 1 kmph” and “experience heavy jerk above 50% of TE LSP may glow some times
[DO]
1. While TE >100KN, after 3 minutes this fault message comes and, If the DDS message is warning loco creeping a. Apply sanders and try to overcome the creeping problem. b. Operate throttle gradually and pick up speed more than 11 kmph. The logic will reset after 11kmph. c. If train stalls or unable to start in up gradient, secure the train and bring throttle to 0 and wait for 2 minutes. d. Ensure there is no brake binding on formation, oil/ grease or wet rails and both converters are in service. e. Operate Throttle gradually duly pressing PSA and try.
2. In GTO SR locos If the following fault messages are recorded with or without ‘warning Loco creeping’ a. ASC1:0052 Error Tacho Generator 1 - TM-1, ASC1:0053 Error Tacho Generator 2 - TM-2, ASC1:0054 Error Tacho Generator 3 - TM-3 b. ASC2:0052 Error Tacho Generator 1 - TM-4, ASC2:0053 Error Tacho Generator 2 - TM-5, ASC2:0054 Error Tacho Generator 3 - TM-6
c. Isolate bogie-1 for ASC1 0052/0053/0054 fault messages by keeping 154 in I position and ensure concern axle rotation. d. Isolate bogie-2 for ASC2 0052/0053/0054 messages by keeping 154 in II position. Do not switch off CE e. Work with one bogie in service if load permits. If unsuccessful, ask for relief / banker loco and record in log book.

@@2 SS02  Traction Bogie 1 (SR1) | ss=2
Fault No Message Lamp Effect Action to be taken by crew

@@3 F 02 01 P1  Disturbance in Converter 1 Try to close VCB again | code=F 02 01 P1
[SEE]
Disturbance in Converter 1 Try to close VCB again
LSDJ glows, LSFI flickers & BPFA glows

@@3 F 02 05 P2  EQUIPMENT TEMPERATURE HIGH | code=F 02 05 P2
[SEE]
EQUIPMENT TEMPERATURE HIGH
BPFA will glow TE/BE Will continuously Reduced.
[DO]
1. Press BPFA.
2. Clear block section. Bring throttle to 0 position.
3. Check MCB 59.1/1 (HB-1), if tripped reset once.
4. If MCB 59.1/1 and 53.1/1 is normal, check the working of OCB1 and TMB1 and for any abnormality ,
5. If any abnormality is noticed, after opening VCB, open MCB 59.1/1 and place154 in 1

@@3 F 02 07 P2  Wheel Skidding in Bogie 1 BPFA will glow | code=F 02 07 P2
[SEE]
Wheel Skidding in Bogie 1 BPFA will glow
[DO]
1. Press BPFA.
2. Reduce braking effort and observe wheel skidding sound in running condition, Clear block section
3. Ensure free rotation of wheels and no brake binding in loco.
4. Ensure that all parking brakes are released fully.
5. Inform TLC and make entry in logbook and work.
6. If speed is more than 1 KMPH, no skidding sound from bogie and there is no fault message in DDS as Error Tacho generator, normal working can be continued.
7. If everything is normal and wheel skidding message repeats with jerk isolate SR1 by placing 154 in I and try.
8. Do not use BPCS and re-generative braking

6. Inform TLC and make entry in logbook and work.
7. If speed is more than 1 KMPH, no skidding sound from bogie and there is no fault message in DDS as Error Tacho Generator. Then LP can work normally.
8. If everything is normal and wheel skidding message repeats with jerk isolate SR 2 by placing 154 in II and try.
9. Do not use BPCS and re-generative braking.
!note Note: In IGBT Loco, if any TM isolation, check DDS. If TM isolation is related to speed sensor failure, LP to check free rotation of wheel of the isolated TM before start and check frequently for skidded wheel in isolated TM.

@@2 SS04  Harmonic Filter | ss=4

@@3 F 04 01 P1  HARMONIC FILTER CURRENT TOO HIGH Try to close VCB again | code=F 04 01 P1
[SEE]
HARMONIC FILTER CURRENT TOO HIGH Try to close VCB again
LSDJ glows, LSFI flickers & BPFA glows
VCB trips 1. Bring throttle to “0” and press BPFA.
2. Close VCB and work onwards.
3. If the fault message comes 2nd time within 30 minutes, Harmonic Filter will isolate automatically. TE/BE Will be available upto 40kmph.(After 40 kmph speed TE or BE cut OFF)
4. Clear section ( TE/BE available upto 40 KMPH) and Do VCU reset (For Switch OFF/ON CE)
5. If unsuccessful, load and road permit, Do VCU reset (For Switch OFF/ON CE) to bring back harmonic filter into service, open VCB, place 154 in 1 position, energise loco and work with single bogie.
6. If load and road does not permit contact TLC and act as per instruction.
7. Record in log book and inform TLC and act accordingly.
Isolation HARMONIC LSDJ Maximum Message FILTER glows, Permitted ISOLATED LSFI TE/BE is 40 Speak to TLC flickers Kmph & Press Enter BPFA glows

@@3 F 04 02 P1  HARMONIC FILTER CONTACTOR (S) STUCK OFF /ON Speak to TLC | code=F 04 02 P1
[SEE]
HARMONIC FILTER CONTACTOR (S) STUCK OFF /ON Speak to TLC
LSDJ & BPFA glows, LSFI flickers
Harmonic filter will be isolated
[DO]
1. Bring throttle to “0” and press BPFA. Harmonic filter will Isolate automatically.
2. In IGBT, CCB2 Loco in case of MR/FP drops due to CRO, attend FP leak, VCU reset to bring back HF to service.
3. Close VCB. TE/BE will be reduced to 40kmph.
4. Ensure filter block cut out cock is open in pneumatic panel and check 127.2/1, (SB1) if tripped, switch OFF CE, reset MCB, and switch ON CE.
5. Clear section (TE/BE available upto 40 KMPH).
6. Do VCU reset (For Switch OFF/ON CE)
7. If Harmonic filter comes into service, resume traction with normal speed.
8. If unsuccessful, open VCB, place 154 in I and switch off MCB 127.1/1.
9. Do VCU reset (For Switch OFF/ON CE) to bring back the isolated filter in to service and work with half TE / BE at normal speed as per instruction of TLC.
10. If Harmonic Filter not comes to service, bring back Bogie1 to service and work with restricted TE/BE (40 Kmph), as per instruction of TLC
11. Record in log book and inform to TLC.
[ISOLATION]
Isolation Message HARMONIC FILTER ISOLATED Speak to TLC Press Enter
LSFI glows Maximum Permitted TE/BE is 40 Kmph

@@3 F 04 04 P1  RESISTOR TOO HOT No. of filter Discharges exceeded VCB will remain inhibited 15 min. | code=F 04 04 P1
[SEE]
RESISTOR TOO HOT No. of filter Discharges exceeded VCB will remain inhibited 15 min.
LSDJ glows, LSFI flickers & BPFA glows
Harmonic Filter will be Isolated. Max TE/BE 40 Kmph
[DO]
1. Bring throttle to “0” and press BPFA.
2. Clear the block section with coasting or stop the train at convenient place.
3. Wait for 15 minutes and close VCB again and resume traction.
4. If unsuccessful, do VCU reset (For Switch OFF/ON CE)
5. Inform TLC and make a remark in log book.

@@3 F 04 01 P2  FILTER CONTACTOR 8.1 STUCK ON, If VCB opens it will not close again | code=F 04 01 P2
[SEE]
FILTER CONTACTOR 8.1 STUCK ON, If VCB opens it will not close again
BPFA Will glow
VCB cannot be closed if VCB opened by LP
[DO]
1. Press BPFA.
2. Try to avoid bringing throttle to 0. Do not open VCB and clear section and stop the train.
3. Open and close VCB. If unable to close VCB again. Do VCU reset (For Switch OFF/ON CE) and try.
4. If unsuccessful troubleshoot for F 01 06 P1.
5. If unsuccessful ask for relief loco and make a remark in log book.

@@3 F 04 02 P2  EARTH FAULT HARMONIC FILTER CIRCUIT | code=F 04 02 P2
[SEE]
EARTH FAULT HARMONIC FILTER CIRCUIT
BPFA Will glow Normal operation can continue To be checked during maintenance
[DO]
1. Press BPFA and resume normal traction.
2. Inform TLC and make a remark in log book Check Filter Block in Machine room for any smoke, smell or any abnormality. If any, switch OFF 127.2/1 and close FB coc in pneumatic panel and clear section , with maximum TE/BE 40 kmph, and If NO abnormality resume traction

!note Note 2: HOG will not work in the following condition.

[DO]

2. Check the HLC front panel status LED's, if fault LED/s are glowing switch OFF HLC control MCB in SB 2 panel and ask PC staff to restart EOG.
3. If HLC is not starting and HLC display showing waiting for ON COMMAND, Ask PC car staff to reconnect IV coupler, and try to start EOG or reset from PC
!note Note 4: If HOG coupler wrongly connected to MU coupler socket, “Reverser defective” or “both CAB occupied” message will be displayed

8. If unsuccessful, isolate BUR3 (Trip MCB 127.22/3 in SB-2 panel after VCB open) and work with BUR 1 and BUR-2 & Inform TLC and record in log book
9. If BUR3 followed by BUR2 or BUR1 isolated, trouble shoot for F 01 10 P1.
!note Note: In case of BUR3 isolated manually or automatically,
1. LP has to check the working of all Blowers by switching OFF TFR-OP MCB (62.1/1), In certain cases due to low temperature blowers may not start.
2. Close VCB, after 40 sec all blowers will start even though Coolant temperature is low.
3. If all the blowers are working, open VCB & normalise 62.1/1 and work with two BURs in service.
4. After Blowers (TMSCB) started working, if BUR1 gets isolated, troubleshoot for F 01 10 P1.

Switch off MCB 127.22/1 to isolate BUR-1. 59.1/2 of OCB-2 4. OCB 1and 2 will work on BUR-II. Note: This problem may arise due to unbalance output voltage
2. If LP experiences 1. Switch off CE repeated tripping of MCB 2. Reset tripped MCB. 63.1/1, 63.1/2, 62.1/1, 62.1/2. 63.1/1 or / and 63.1/2 of converter Oil Pump 1and 2

2. Reset MCB 53.1/1 or/and 53.1/2.

4. TMB 1and 2 will work on BUR-I.
!note Note: This problem may arise due to unbalance output voltage

3. Switch off 127.22/3 to isolate BUR- III.

!note Note: In case of MCB 55.1/1 or/and 55.1/2 (of SCTM-1 or / and SCTM-2) tripping, do not reset the same and work on wards.

@@2 SS09  Battery System | ss=9

@@3 F 09 01 P1  BATTERY LSDJ Protective | code=F 09 01 P1
[SEE]
BATTERY LSDJ Protective
[DO]
1. Bring throttle to “0” and press BPFA.
2. Clear section by coasting if possible or stop the train at convenient place.
3. If MCB 110 in SB-2 panel tripped switch OFF CE and reset MCB110 and switch ON CE.
4. Check MCB 100 (HB-2 or on BUR Box2) if tripped reset the same after opening VCB.
5. If BA voltage is above 82 volts, Check the DDS message, if fault message is generated by BUR-II and then try by isolating BUR-II.
6. In BHEL IGBT BUR Loco ensure the working of Charger, after isolation of BUR3. If Charger not working inform TLC.
7. If fault message is generated by BUR-III and actual voltage in UBA is above 82 volts, then try by isolating BUR-III
8. Use minimum lights, if BA voltage increases, try to energize the loco
9. If battery voltage is less than 82 volts, then contact TLC for relief loco.
10. Make a remark in the logbook
VOLTAGE glows, Shut down TOO LSFI LOW flickers Electronics & Will BPFA switch off glows
UBA shows less than 82V
[ISOLATION]
Isolation Message MAIN POWER ISOLATED VCB cannot be closed: VCB inhabited Loco is dead
LSDJ LSFI Will glow
VCB will not close

@@3 F 09 02 P1  BATTERY VOLTAGE LOW LSFI, Flickers & BPFA will glow | code=F 09 02 P1
[SEE]
BATTERY VOLTAGE LOW LSFI, Flickers & BPFA will glow
Converters being switched off
[DO]
1. Bring throttle to “0” and press BPFA.
2. Clear section by coasting if possible or stop the train at convenient place.
3. If MCB 100 (HB2 OR on BUR BOX 2) is tripped open VCB and reset.
4. If MCB 110(in SB2 panel) tripped switch OFF CE and reset MCB 110 and switch ON CE.
[ISOLATION]
Isolation MAIN POWER ISOLATED VCB cannot be closed: VCB inhabited Loco is dead
LSDJ, LSFI will glow
VCB will not close
5. If BA voltage is above 82 volts, loco to be kept energized for battery charging.
6. If MCB 100 repeatedly tripping, isolate BUR-III by opening MCB 127.22/3 in SB2 after opening VCB.
7. Now MCB 100 is providing on ABB BUR also, so check that also in ABB BUR.
7. Inform TLC and make a remark in the logbook
8. If the battery can’t be switched ON or below 82 volts ask for relief loco.

@@3 F 09 03 P1  PANTOGRAPH LOWERED LONGER THAN 10 MINUTES | code=F 09 03 P1
[SEE]
PANTOGRAPH LOWERED LONGER THAN 10 MINUTES
MEMO TEL LIGHT S OFF
Control electronics will switch off
[DO]
1. Press BPFA.
2. If BL key is at D and pantograph is lowered or not raising or OHE out of range longer than 10 minutes, then this fault message is expected.
3. Switch ON the CE once again by BL Key if required. Raise pantograph within 10 minutes after activating the cab.

@@3 F 09 01 P2  WARNING: LOW BATTERY VOLTAGE Check battery charger MCB | code=F 09 01 P2
[SEE]
!note WARNING: LOW BATTERY VOLTAGE Check battery charger MCB
BPFA will glow
Driving available for less than30 min
[DO]
1. Press BPFA
2. If MCB 100 (HB-2 or BUR Box-2) is tripped open VCB and reset.
3. If battery charger output MCB 110 in SB-2 is tripped, switch OFF CE and reset. Energise loco and work on wards.
4. Inform TLC and make a remark in logbook
5. Now MCB 100 is providing on ABB BUR also, so check that also in ABB BUR.

@@3 F 09 02 P2  BATTERY CHARGER MCB OFF Try to close the MCB | code=F 09 02 P2
[SEE]
BATTERY CHARGER MCB OFF Try to close the MCB
BPFA will glow Battery not being charged
[DO]
1. Press BPFA
2. Check the availability of MCB100.
3. If MCB 100 (HB-2 or on BUR Box-2) is tripped open VCB and reset.
4. If MCB-100 tripping repeatedly then isolate BUR-3 through MCB 127.22/3 in SB-2 work with CHBA on BUR 2.
5. If unsuccessful, Switch off/on CE and try.
6. Whenever voltage reaches 92V clear section and contact TLC.
!note Note: In ABB make IGBT BUR fitted locos, instead of MCB 100, HRC fuses are provided inside BUR BOX -2.

@@3 F 09 03 P2  LOW BATTERY CHARGER CURRENT Battery charger MCB may have Tripped | code=F 09 03 P2
[SEE]
LOW BATTERY CHARGER CURRENT Battery charger MCB may have Tripped
BPFA will glow Battery not being charged
[DO]
1. Press BPFA
2. Check battery charger output MCB 110 in SB-2, if tripped switch off CE and reset. Ensure MCB100 (HB2 OR on BUR BOX-2 is normal.
3. If MCB 100 and 110 are normal and UBA reads more voltage after closing VCB indicates battery is charging, ignore the fault message and resume normal operation.
4. Inform TLC and make a remark in the log book.
5. Now MCB 100 is provided on ABB BUR also, so check that also in ABB BUR.

BPFA Will glow No more faults will store in DDS

1. Press BPFA
2. Continue normal traction.
3. Make a remark in the logbook.
4. Inform TLC during next stop.

BPFA will glow Nothing To be checked during maintenance

1. Press BPFA
2. Continue normal traction.
3. Inform TLC and make a remark in the logbook.

@@2 SS10  Brake System | ss=10

@@3 F 10 01 P1  Fault in Brake Electronics Full service Brake Applied | code=F 10 01 P1
[SEE]
Fault in Brake Electronics Full service Brake Applied
LSFI Flickers & BPFA Will glow TE/BE comes to ‘0’
Emergency brake applied No traction allowed
[DO]
1. Bring throttle to “0” and press BPFA.
2. Check MCB 127.7 in SB2. If tripped, Open VCB and reset once and try.
3. Release parking brake through SV30 release plunger, avoid the operation of sanders and work onwards. If unsuccessful try from rear cab.
4. If MCB is not tripped, switch OFF/ON CE and try. (Through BL key only)
5. If the same fault message repeats, ask for relief loco and record in log book.
6. In KNORR brake system, check EBV display fault code and Troubleshoot as per annexure.
7. In KNORR brake loco ensure rear cab A9 in FS and mode switch in Trail position.
8. If unsuccessful clear section with PTDC mode, (if available) and as per the instructions to make PTDC mode.
PTDC MODE:
1. Switch OFF 127.7 IN SB2 Panel.
2. Turn PER-COS from Horizontal to Vertical. Press BPFA.
3. Keep PTDC handle to release (towards backward) position to charge BP. Ensure rear CAB PTDC handle is in Middle position.
4. Release Brake cylinder pressure by C3WDV/QRV (KE-valve) or by pressing TP16/TP20 nipples. (Keep wooden wedges to avoid roll back on gradient section)
5. Move Throttle, ensure the Node FLG 596, both TE/BE meter deviates and KN in display.
6. Clear section with Speed not exceeding 10 kmph.
!note Note: In case of LE, ensure conjunction brake is working. SA9 will not work, A9 works in Emergency position only.

@@3 F 10 02 P1  LOW PRESSURE MAIN RESERVOIR | code=F 10 02 P1
[SEE]
LOW PRESSURE MAIN RESERVOIR
LSFI Flickers & BPFA Will glow TE/BE comes to‘0’
No traction allowed till pressure reaches 6.4 kg/cm²
[DO]
1. Bring throttle to “0” and press BPFA. Stop the train if MR pressure drops and place MPJ in 0.
2. Check the working of both MCP, if not working press BLCP to MAN position and try.
3. Check the MCB 47.1/1 in HB1 and MCB 47.1/2 in HB2 panel. If tripped, reset the MCB after opening VCB
4. If MCB are normal, switch OFF/ON CE and try.
5. If unsuccessful, isolate BUR3 (open 127.22/3 in SB2 panel) and try.
6. Try from rear cab. If successful clear section.
7. If compressors are working and pressure is not building up, BP pressure leakage causes drop in MR pressure.
8. Isolate VCD/SPM / TPWS (close COC both cabs below A9). Ensure /ALP emergency closed on both cabs and rear cab A9 in neutral position.
9. If FP pressure drops, close 136 COC and try
10. If air leaks through Unloader valve, close the isolating COC for un-loaders near BA box 2 and above MCP1. After MR charging, open the COC and try. Ensure MR1, MR2 and AR drain cocs in closed condition
11. Any air leakage in the front side, close corresponding additional angle COC in under frame and work on wards.
12. If there no leakage from air dryers and unloader valves, bypass air dryer. Close D IN and D OUT and open D OFF cocs.
13. If MR pressure is available and fault message appears, gently tap RGLP pressure switch (269.4) near AR tank.
14. Ensure Hotel load working, if available.
15. Inform TLC and make a remark in the logbook

@@3 F 10 03 P1  VIGILANCE EMERGENCY BRAKE APPLICATION | code=F 10 03 P1
[SEE]
VIGILANCE EMERGENCY BRAKE APPLICATION
LSVW &BPFA will glow, LSFI flickers TE/BE comes to ‘0’ Buzzer
Emergency brake BP pressure will drop to 3 to 2.5 kg/cm² Buzzer will sound
[DO]
1. Bring throttle to 0.
2. Keep A9 in emergency position, apply SA9 and keep MPJ-0
3. Wait for 120 seconds in E70 loco and 32 seconds in KNORR (CCB 2) loco.
4. Press BPVR to reset VCD, LSVW will extinguish and buzzer will stop.
5. In case of KNORR brake (CCB1.5) system loco keep A-9 in FS and press BPVR, after 120 seconds.
6. Press BPFA.
7. Keep A9 handle on run position and wait for recharging of BP pressure.
8. Start the train as per procedure.
9. If VCD is malfunctioning, isolate VCD by keeping 237.1 in ‘0’ (SB-1). If unsuccessful switch OFF/ON CE (Through BL key) and try
10. Work without VCD and resume normal traction and be vigilant.
11. Inform to TLC and make a remark in logbook.

@@3 F 10 04 P1  WORKING WRONG CONFIGURATION BRAKE SYSTEM | code=F 10 04 P1
[SEE]
WORKING WRONG CONFIGURATION BRAKE SYSTEM
LSFI Flickers and BPFA will glow
Traction not possible
[DO]
1. Bring throttle to 0.
2. In KNORR, ensure MODE switch is in LEAD in working CAB
3. Check isolating cock No. 70 in pneumatic panel and ZBAN OFF condition (A Panel).
4. Open VCB and normalise 70 COC. Press BPFA and resume normal traction.
5. If unsuccessful, change cab after switching OFF/ON CE (Through BL key) and try
6. Inform to TLC and make a remark in logbook.

@@3 F 10 05 P1  TRACTION WITH AUTO BRAKE NOT ALLOWED | code=F 10 05 P1
[SEE]
TRACTION WITH AUTO BRAKE NOT ALLOWED
LSFI Flickers and BPFA glows
Auto brake applied TE/BE not possible.
[DO]
1. Bring throttle 0 and apply brake if required. Press BPFA.
2. If loco is in traction ensure A9 in run position and BP 5 Kg/cm 2 .
3. If all operation is normal and fault repeat switch OFF/ON CE (Through BL key) and try
4. Try from rear cab. Inform to TLC and make a remark in logbook.

@@3 F 10 06 P1  TRACTION WITH PARKING BRAKES NOT ALLOWED | code=F 10 06 P1
[SEE]
TRACTION WITH PARKING BRAKES NOT ALLOWED
LSFI flickers & BPFA glows
Parking brake applied
TE/BE not possible
[DO]
1. Bring throttle to 0.
2. Press BPPB and release PB. Ensure PB gauge shows pressure of 6 Kg/cm 2 . Press BPFA
3. If parking brake is not released then check the SV30 on pneumatic panel.
4. If it is permanently latched in applied condition then release and lock it.
5. If still parking brake not released, check for any sign of air leakage from parking brake cylinder hosepipe.
6. Release Parking brakes manually in all 4 wheels by gently pulling manual release handle. (In WAG9-Wheel 2,6,7,11 and in WAP5 Wheel-1,4,5,8. Before manual release ensure PB and BC gauges shows ‘0’ Kg/cm 2 )
7. If there is leakage, try to arrest the leakage.
8. After releasing parking brake, Press BPFA and resume normal traction.
9. Inform to TLC and make a remark in logbook

@@3 F 10 07 P1  REGENERATIVE BRAKE FAILURE | code=F 10 07 P1
[SEE]
REGENERATIVE BRAKE FAILURE
LSFI Flickers & BPFA glows
Brake will apply to loco.
[DO]
1. Bring throttle to ‘’0’’ position
2. During RGB, if fails (VCB opens other than BLDJ) loco brakes applied according to RG demand. In KNORR, Service penalty brakes applied, if brake cylinder pressure exceeds 2.25 Kg/cm 2
3. Release Loco brake and control the train through A-9.
4. No regenerative brake power available. Braking can be done with A9 and SA9.
5. Resume normal traction. Inform to TLC and make a remark in logbook.

@@3 F 10 08 P1  EMERGENCY STOP, SHUTDOWN ON THE LOCO | code=F 10 08 P1
[SEE]
EMERGENCY STOP, SHUTDOWN ON THE LOCO
LSFI Flickers and BPFA glows
VCB open, pantograp h lower, TE/BE ‘0’, Emergency brake will apply
[DO]
1. Bring throttle to 0.
2. Reset emergency stop push button (BPEMS) by rotating it in the direction of arrow mark on it. It will project out. Press BPFA.
3. Raise pantograph. Close VCB. Resume normal traction after charging BP and MR pressure.
4. If unsuccessful try from rear cab.
!note Note: If emergency stop button is pressed before energising, and on moving BL to D mode FLG71 protection activated message will appear. Reset stop button.

@@3 F 10 09 P1  TRACTION NOT ALLOWED WITH APPLIED BRAKES | code=F 10 09 P1
[SEE]
TRACTION NOT ALLOWED WITH APPLIED BRAKES
LSFI Flickers & BPFA glow
Loco brake is applied
[DO]
1. Bring throttle to 0. This fault message is generated when BC pressure is more than 0.65 Kg/cm 2 and throttle is not at zero position and speed is over 10 kmph or during alarm chain pulling (BP drop more than 0.6 kg/cm 2 ) with the above conditions .
3. Ensure throttle is at 0 during brake application.
4. Gently tap pressure switches No. 269.6/1 and 269.6/2 located on the side wall near AR tank if BC reads 0 pressure.
5. Release SA9 and C3W DV & Ensure rear cab SA9 in release.
6. In KNORR, check EBV display. If it is Train line emergency and BP ‘0’, check LED on SIFA magnet valve. LED glows- emergency exhaust normal, attend formation. LED extinguished- attend Loco.
7. Lift bail off ring on SA9, press 20TP and 16 TP nipples in pneumatic panel in KNORR brake loco
8. Ensure BP pressure is at 5 Kg/cm 2.
9. Press BPFA and resume traction
10. Still unsuccessful switch OFF/ON CE (BL key) and try.
11. Inform TLC and record in log book.

@@3 F 10 10 P1  EMG. EXHAUST COCK CLOSED, NO TRACTION | code=F 10 10 P1
[SEE]
EMG. EXHAUST COCK CLOSED, NO TRACTION
LSFI Flickers & BPFA glows
BP is reduced to ‘0’
[DO]
1. Bring throttle to 0.
2. Open the emergency exhaust COC 74 in the pneumatic panel.
3. If 74 COC is already open and fault message appears, the problem may be with micro switch of 74 COC.
4. Operate 74 COC manually 2 or 3 times. If successful, press BPFA and proceed further.
5. If unsuccessful, switch OFF and ON CE (Through BL key) and try.
6. Inform to TLC and make a remark in logbook.

@@3 F 10 01 P2  LOCO IS IN BANKING MODE | code=F 10 01 P2
[SEE]
LOCO IS IN BANKING MODE
BPFA Will glow Loco brake controller isolated Emergency brakes can be applied
[DO]
1. This fault message will appear when loco is set up in banking mode operation. Press BPFA.
2. If loco in normal mode, bring throttle to “ 0”
3. Ensure ZBAN is OFF.
4. Ensure 70 COC is open/MODE switch in LEAD, Open VCB and operate 2 or 3 times and try.
5. If unsuccessful, change cab after switching OFF MCE and try.
6. Inform TLC and record in log book.

@@3 F 10 02 P2  ALARM CHAIN PULLING Check train | code=F 10 02 P2
[SEE]
ALARM CHAIN PULLING Check train
BPFA & LSAF will glow
BP drops along with AFM over shoots
[DO]
1. If AFI overshoot and BP pressure drops, switch on flasher immediately.
2. This message generated only when TE/BE in ‘0’. If it is in Traction, message will come as F 10 09 P1.
3. In KNORR check EBV display since No front end message appears.
4. Stop the train at convenient place immediately.
5. Check the affected coach/wagon for BP leakage, find out the reason of ACP, reset the ACP and work as per G&SR.
6. If AFI overshoots in twin pipe system check AR drain coc of affected coach.
7. In case a conventional Dead Loco attached, isolate C3WDV and try
8. After recreating BP pressure and AFI comes to normal, restart the train as per procedure. 9.Inform TLC and record in log book.
No fault message, but if LP experiences that air drier is not working
Check MCB 128.1, if found tripped, reset the MCB once. If the MCB trips again, resume normal operation with air drier in isolated (Switch OFF 128.1) condition. Also record in the log book
The procedure to check ACP apparatus: (AFL Buzzer and Flasher light will not work while Loco is in standstill condition)
1. In E70 AFI shoot up is the indication for ACP.
2. In KNORR CCB2 display, ‘Train line emergency’ is the indication for ACP

2. Recycle CCB system (Put OFF Pneumatic panel MCB/127.7 inside SB2 Panel and again turn ON after 10 sec) or Switch off /ON CE and try.
3. If problem is not set, then operate locomotive in PTDC mode to clear the block section. 125 MV26 De- energized
1. Move working cab Auto Handle rapidly to Full Service and back to RUN position several times and ensure fault is cleared.
2. If problem is set, ensure BC development by Auto (A9) and Independent (SA9), Work Locomotive further or Switch off /ON CE and try.
3. If problem not set, then, Work in PTDC mode from Active Cab and clear Block section 136 16CP BPT Fault 1. Ensure LON Cable Connector is properly seated/locked on 16CP and J-103 connector on PSJB.
2. Recycle CCB system (Put OFF Pneumatic panel MCB/127.7 inside SB2 Panel and again turn ON after 10 sec)
3. Automatic BC pressure may be direct release only. No blending or other special BC control will be active.
4. If problem not set, then, Work in PTDC mode from Active Cab and clear Block section
138 ACMV Fault 1. Move working cab Auto Handle rapidly to RELEASE and back to RUN position several times and ensure fault is cleared.
2. Ensure LON Cable Connector is properly seated/locked on BPCP and J-103 connector on PSJB.
3. Recycle CCB system (Put OFF Pneumatic panel MCB/127.7 inside SB2 Panel and again turn ON after 10 sec) If problem is not set, ensure BC development by Auto (A9) and Independent (SA9), inform shed & Work Locomotive further.
4. Over-charge feature not working, Locomotive can work without any problem
140 MR/FL Dual Transducer Fault
1. Check for any excess MR pressure, Drain MR pressure & see whether fault gets cleared.
2. Ensure LON Cable Connector is properly seated/locked on BPCP and J-103 connector on PSJB.
3. Recycle CCB system (Put OFF Pneumatic panel MCB/127.7 inside SB2 Panel and again turn ON after 10 sec)
4. If problem is not set, then operate locomotive in PTDC mode to clear the block section.
141 LCI Comm Loss 1. Ensure LON Cable Connector J4 is properly seated/locked on MPIO.
2. Recycle CCB system (Put OFF Pneumatic panel MCB/127.7 inside SB2 Panel and again turn ON after 10 sec) or Switch off /ON CE and try.
3. If problem is not set, then operate locomotive in PTDC mode to clear the block section. 142 20 BMV Fault 1. Switch off /ON CE and try.
146 EBV Operating Mode Switch change at speed
1. Recover applied Penalty Brake. Check position of Lead/Trail switches: Active cab Switch in LEAD position & Inactive Cab Switch in TRAIL position.
2. If problem is not set, then Put OFF Pneumatic panel MCB/127.7 inside SB2 Panel & disconnect Inactive Cab EBV connector.
3. Ensure BC development by Auto (A9) and Independent (SA9) from Active Cab, inform shed & Work Locomotive further.

@@2 SS11  Aux HB 1 | ss=11

@@3 F 11 01 P2  MCB(S) BPFA Traction | code=F 11 01 P2
[SEE]
MCB(S) BPFA Traction
[DO]
1. Press BPFA.
2. Check all MCB in HB-1.
3. If any MCB tripped, open VCB and reset once, close VCB and resume normal traction,
4. If OCB-1 or SR-1 pump or TMB-1 MCB trips again, do not reset, open VCB keep 154 on I position.
5. If transformer oil pump MCB is tripped again do not reset, work further. TE/BE will be reduced to 70% automatically.
6. In case of MRB-1 MCB tripping repeatedly contact TLC and act as per instructions of TLC.
7. If no MCB is tripped, but still fault message is coming and all auxiliaries are working, ignore the fault message and resume normal traction.
8. Oil pumps/OCB/ TMBs MCBs, alternatively tripping troubleshoot as per general instruction of SS06, SS07, SS08
9. Inform TLC and enter in to log book.
TRIPPED IN will power may AUX. glow reduced, if CUBICLE 1 temperature (HB1 PANEL) exceed.

[SEE]
EARTH FAULT 415/110V CIRCUIT
BPFA will glow Normal operation can continue
[DO]
1. Resume normal traction.
2. Check all MCBs in HB1 and HB2 panels.
3. Ensure the working of MRB1&2
4. Inform TLC and make a remark in logbook. (To be checked during maintenance)

@@3 F 11 03 P2  HB1 BPFA MCP1 not available | code=F 11 03 P2
[SEE]
HB1 BPFA MCP1 not available
[DO]
1. Check MCB 47.1/1 (HB1). If tripped, open VCB and reset once.
2. Close VCB and resume normal traction.
3. If this MCB trips again then work the train with one compressor.
4. Ensure MCP1 un-loader valve cock above MCP- 1 or BA box 2 is open.
5. If 47.1/1 and 47.1/2 alternatively tripping, Open VCB, reset MCB and isolate BUR3 and try.
6. Inform TLC and make a remark in logbook
MCB OF will MAIN COMPRESSOR OPEN
glow

@@3 F 12 01 P2  MCB(S) BPFA Traction power may | code=F 12 01 P2
[SEE]
MCB(S) BPFA Traction power may
[DO]

2. If any MCB tripped, open VCB and reset once, close VCB and resume normal traction,
3. If OCB-2 or SR-2 pump or TMB-2 MCB trips again place, open VCB, keep 154 on II position and work with 50% of TE/BE.
4. If transformer oil pump MCB is tripped again work further.
5. TE/BE will be reduced to 70% automatically.

7. If no MCB is tripped, but still fault message is coming and all auxiliaries are working ignore the fault message and resume normal traction.
8. Ensure 127.2/2 in SB-2 is normal. If this MCB is tripped HB-2 panel MCB tripped fault message will appear.
9. Oil pumps/OCB alternatively tripping troubleshoot as per general instruction of SS06, SS07, SS08
10. Inform TLC and enter in to log book

@@2 SS15  Fire Detection | ss=15
Fault No Message Lamp Effect Action to be taken by crew

@@3 F 15 01 P1  FIRE IN MACHINE ROOM Extinguish the fire Reset the FDU | code=F 15 01 P1
[SEE]
FIRE IN MACHINE ROOM Extinguish the fire Reset the FDU
LSFI Flickers & BPFA will glow Buzzer sounds
TE/BE comes to 0
[DO]
1. Open VCB, lower pantograph.
2. Inspect both the machine rooms.
3. Check all MCB in HB1/2, SB1/2 panels and its equipment for smell/smoke and do not reset if tripped. Work the train further with precaution.
4. If there is any smoke emission /fire stop the train immediately.
5. Switch OFF the CE and Switch OFF MCB112.1.
6. Extinguish the fire by using portable fire extinguisher in case of any sign of smoke emission.
7. If any smoke from Filter Block, isolate HF by switch OFF 127.2/1 and clear section with 40 kmph of TE/BE
8. In case of severe fire especially in traction converter, use fixed fire extinguisher duly closing both machine room doors, (Open valve, ensure gauge then open coc)
9. After extinguishing the fire contact TLC.
10. Switch OFF OCB 1&2, TMB 1&2 and all 4 scavenger blowers MCBs and MRB 1&2 MCB, Close VCB. If any abnormal smell/smoke ask for relief loco.
11. If there is no abnormalities even after taking TE. Normalise MCB one by one and act accordingly.
12. If no fire and everything is normal, reset the FDU by pressing reset push button provided on FDU unit located in SB-2 cubicle, Press BPFA and resume normal traction.
13. Inform to TLC and make a remark in logbook.
RESET BUTTON IN FDU
SB2 PANEL

@@3 F 15 01 P2  FAULT IN FIRE DETECTION UNIT | code=F 15 01 P2
[SEE]
FAULT IN FIRE DETECTION UNIT
BPFA will glow
No fire detection Possible. Normal operation can continue
[DO]
1. Press BPFA
2. Keep on checking the machine rooms as frequently as possible for the sign of any smoke / fire.
3. Resume normal traction.
4. Inform to TLC and make a remark in logbook.

@@3 F 15 02 P2  WARNING SMOKE IN MACHINE ROOM | code=F 15 02 P2
[SEE]
!note WARNING SMOKE IN MACHINE ROOM
BPFA will glow Buzzer sounds
Check Machine Room
[DO]
1. Open VCB, lower pantograph
2. Inspect both the machine rooms.
3. Check all MCB in HB1and 2, SB1and 2 panels and its equipment for smell/smoke and do not reset if tripped. Work the train further.
4. If there is any smoke emission /fire stop the train immediately. Switch OFF the CE and trip MCB112.1.
5. Extinguish the fire by using portable fire extinguisher in case of any sign of smoke emission.
6. In case of severe fire especially in traction converter, use fixed fire extinguisher duly closing both machine room doors. (Open valve, ensure gauge then open coc)
7. After extinguishing the fire contact TLC for advice.
8. If no fire and everything is normal, reset the fire detection unit by pressing reset push button provided on FDU unit located in SB-2
9. Press BPFA and resume normal traction. Inform to TLC and make a remark in logbook
!note Note: If any smoke or fire in Hotel load converter Manual Fire Extinguishing only to be done.

@@2 SS16  Speedometer | ss=16

@@3 F 16 01 P1  SPEED LIMIT EXCEEDED | code=F 16 01 P1
[SEE]
SPEED LIMIT EXCEEDED
(10% MORE THAN MPS)
LSFI Flickers & BPFA will glow Buzzer sound
Emergency brakes TE/BE ‘0’
[DO]
1. Bring throttle to 0 to reduce loco speed.
2. If the speed shown in speedometer is not coming within the limits, but actual speed has come down this means that speedometer is defective.
3. Switch OFF speedometer MCB 127.92 provided in SB2 panel. Now the speedometer is isolated
4. Drive carefully by watching speed in display in simulation screen.
5. Inform TLC and make a remark in the logbook.

@@3 F 16 01 P2  Fault in Speedometer BPFA will glow buzzer sounds | code=F 16 01 P2
[SEE]
Fault in Speedometer BPFA will glow buzzer sounds
No display of speed in the cab Drive carefully, Use Simulation Screen
[DO]
1. Check the speedometer MCB 127.92 in SB2. If it is tripped, reset the MCB once and resume normal operation.
2. If fault still existing, isolate the speedometer by switching OFF the MCB 127.92 provided in SB2. Now the speedometer will be isolated.
3. Drive carefully by watching the speed in display in simulation screen.
4. Inform TLC and make a remark in the logbook. Resume normal traction as per instruction of TLC.
