@@4 F 19 01 P1  COMMUNICATION LSDJ Multiple | code=F 19 01 P1
[SEE]
COMMUNICATION LSDJ Multiple
[DO]
1. If fault message comes in Single Loco, acknowledge through BPFA.
2. LSFI will glow continuously and resume normal traction.
3. In case of MU loco, try to clear section with leading loco and stop the train convenient place (clear section within 10 minutes. Otherwise rear loco CE OFF and BP will drop.)
4. Switch off CE in both loco. Check UIC cable (MU cable in between loco) for any disconnection or slack. If any abnormality, connect it properly.
5. Energise both locos as per procedure and work onwards if normal.
DISTURBANCE glows operation
Try to close the VCB LSFI Flickers &
not possible
Again BPFA will
glow
[ISOLATION]
Isolation TRAIN BUS LSDJ Slave 1. Open VCB and lower pantograph of both loco.
2. Switch OFF CE of both locos.
3. Switch OFF 112.1 in SB-2 of slave Loco.
4. Detach the UIC MU cable between locos and keep it in safe custody, Work with Single loco, if load/road permits.
Message ISOLATED LSFI Loco will not be
glows Working.

@@1 Common Problems & Procedures | icon=build | sub=Pantograph, VCB, headlight, brakes, BP, MU, DPWCS…

@@2 5  Important points to avoid loco failure / detention during troubleshooting
1. As soon as a fault message comes, Note down the fault number with Loco number in rough journal.
2. Then troubleshoot according to the fault number with reference to TSD.
3. To view back ground Message press HOME button and select PROCESS INFORMATION and press ENTER, then select COMMISSIONING DATA and press ENTER, select DIAGNOSTIC DATA SET and press ENTER. Only with the details of back ground messages for particular fault, Contact TLC/SSE for advice,
4. While on run, if bogie meter suddenly not responding and DDS reads TE in KN, bring throttle to 0, trip VCB and reclose VCB.
5. BPCS switch should not be pressed for more than 5 second. It shall give stuck up message and become non-functional.
6. Ensure sanders are in working order, sand boxes filled with sand and spare sand bags are available. Inform TLC about any deficiency in this regard to avoid enroute detention.
7. If CE unable to switch OFF or unable to energise the loco, after attaching to power car(HOG), switch off Hotel load MCB (129.1 in SB-2) and try. If unsuccessful, remove Hotel load ‘UIC’ coupler and try. After energizing the Loco normalize the Hotel load and work further.
8. While working IGBT Locos, for isolating Bogie 1, switch OFF 127.1/1 and 127.11/1 (SB1 panel). For isolating Bogie 2 switch OFF 127.1/2 and 127.11/2 (SB2 panel).

@@2 6  Miscellaneous troubles without fault messages

@@3 6.1  When BL key moved from OFF to D, Control Electronics not getting ON
!h Without indication lamps (Ensure 112.1 in SB2 panel ON).
Check marker lights (ensure 310.7 ‘ON’), if not glowing check MCB 112 in Battery box 2. If tripped reset once and try. If marker lights glows check 127.81 MCB, if tripped, reset the same after switch OFF 112.1.
If 127.81 normal, check 126 contactor in SB1 panel. If not closed, gently tap blocking diodes in SB1 and SB2 panel and try, if unsuccessful manually press the contactor 126 for one minute and release. Refer page number 151.
!h With indication lamps are glowing.
Check MCBs in SB1 and SB2 panels. If tripped reset same. If all MCBs are normal, check 218 contactor in SB1 panel. If not closed, gently tap blocking diode in SB1 and SB2 panel and try, if unsuccessful manually press the contactor 218 for one minute and release.
!h Note: If unsuccessful try from rear cab in both occasions.

@@3 6.2  Pantograph not raising without message.
On pressing ZPT, if node remains at 504, ensure 127.12 in SB1 is ON and press manually 130.1 contactor in SB2 panel. If unsuccessful try from rear cab.

@@3 6.3  VCB not closing without message.
On pressing BLDJ, if node remains at 550, press manually 136.4 contactor in SB1 panel. If unsuccessful try from rear cab.

@@2 7  Head light not glowing

@@3 7.1  When both head lights are not glowing while working from cab 1:
1. Check 310.1/1 provided in SB1 panel, if tripped, reset it and work further.
2. If 338/1 (Head light contactor) not picking up after switching ON “BLPR”, wedge the same in closed condition.
3. If still unsuccessful, open head light cover and change the bulbs from rear headlight.

@@3 7.2  When both head lights is not glowing while working from cab2:

@@3 7.3  Indication lamps not glowing and loco working normal from cab-1
!h leading.
Check MCB 127.91/1, if tripped reset once directly and try. If unsuccessful, VCD Buzzer will not work. Isolate VCD and continue traction by watching meters, gauges and fault display and inform to TLC.

@@3 7.4  Indication lamps not glowing and loco working normal from cab-2
!h leading.
Check MCB 127.91/2, if tripped reset once directly and try. If unsuccessful, VCD buzzer will not work and LSVW will not glow from both cabs operation. Isolate VCD and continue traction by watching meters, gauges and fault display and inform to TLC.

@@3 7.5  Checking Battery voltage and CHBA voltage.
LP/shunting or outgoing LP, while Energise/TOC and ON Run, check Battery voltage (UBA), by Open VCB, and check CHBA voltage after closing VCB.

@@2 8  Brakes not releasing / BP not charging

@@3 8.1  Loco brakes not releasing
!h In E-70 system SA-9.
1. Operate SA-9 for 2 to 3 times. Ensure rear cab SA9 in released condition.
2. Switch off /on CE and try. (Through BL key)
3. If still unsuccessful, close 94 COC, place wooden wedges then close both BC cocs to release binding. After pressure released open BC cocs, now conjunction brake only available. Make a remark in log book.
!h In E-70 system A-9.
1. Ensure BP is 5 kg/cm 2 .
2. Pull the C3W Distributor valve quick release valve.
3. Isolate C3W valve and release quick release valve and try.
4. If still unsuccessful, close bogie COC and normalise after BC pressure released. Make remark in logbook.
!h In knorr brake Loco.
Lift bail off ring and try. Press 20TP and 16 TP nipples and release ‘C3WDV’ in pneumatic panel and try. (Do NOT disturb 94 coc in CCB2)

@@3 8.2  BP not charging.
S.No E-70 system CCB 2 system CCB 1.5 system 1  Ensure A9 on A. run position leading cab B. neutral position in trail cab  ZBAN in OFF position. If it is on position, Switch off ZBAN.  In case of Brake Electronics failure message comes Check 127.7 MCB, If 127.7 (SB2) trips, reset once.
 Observe Brake Controller LCD display messages and follow in case of penalty.  If display blanks check 127.7, if 127.7 trips, reset once.  Ensure cab set up is OK.(A9 handle locked in FS in rear Cab and Mode switch in Lead position in Lead cab and Trail Position in rear Cab)  If BP not charging beyond 0 it may be due to A9 rear cab handle in emergency position.
 Ensure Mode switch is in lead position in leading cab and trail in rear cab. Rear cab A9 handle in full service and locked condition. PTDC handle in position-III (Centre).  Check 127.7 MCB, If 127.7 (SB2) trips, reset once.  If BP not charging beyond 0 it may be due to A9 rear cab handle in emergency position. 2  ALP emergency brake handle are in normal condition and both side angle cocks closed condition.  If any air leakage from emergency exhaust Valve (near ALP side footsteps), operate A9 to emergency and try
 ALP Emergency brake handle in both cabs and BP Angle COC are closed)
 ALP emergency brake handle are in normal condition and both side angle cocks closed condition
3  Ensure TPWS COC in both cabs is closed.  Ensure 70 COC open, operate 2-3 times and try  Ensure 74 COC in open  Ensure 136 COC open  Ensure 47 COC closed
 Ensure A. 47 COC is closed (Out), B. 136 COC open (Horizontal), C. 74 COC open (Vertical) and D. PER-COS in closed (Horizontal) condition.  Press test points in BP-CP valve area and try.
 Ensure Parking brakes are in released condition, if not release the same.  If Parking brake is not released, BP will not charge more than 3Kg/cm 2
 PER COS in horizontal position.  Penalty brakes will apply if any one of PB-PDS, PB-BUS, PB-R- COS is isolated normalize the same.  Keep A-9 from RUN to FS. Wait for 10 seconds; then again keep it to RUN.
4  If air leakage noticed from ‘Z port’ (below Pneumatic panel and above bogie wheel no.7) due to emergency valve (71). Reset VCD if acted and try.
 Operate A9 to emergency 2 or 3 times (okay to Run) and try.  Close and open 74 COC 2 or 3 times and try.  Acknowledge the fault message through BPFA.
 If air leakage below pneumatic panel and above bogie MR1 side is due to emergency valve (21). Reset VCD if acted.  Keep A-9 to emergency for 10 seconds and again keep to RUN position and try. Close and open 74 COC 2 to 3 times and try. Acknowledge the fault message through BPFA.
5  Change the cab and try  if unsuccessful, trip 127.92 and try.  If unsuccessful, place 237.1 in 0 and try.  Switch OFF/ON CE and try.  If again unsuccessful clear section with PTDC mode operation if available and other wise contact TLC for advice.
 Change the cab and try  If unsuccessful, trip 127.92 and try.  If unsuccessful, place 237.1 in 0 and try.  Switch OFF/ON CE and try.  If again unsuccessful clear section with PTDC mode operation.
 Change the cab and try  If unsuccessful, trip 127.92 and try.  If unsuccessful, place 237.1 in 0 and try.  Switch OFF/ON CE and try.  If unsuccessful, clear section with PTDC mode and inform to TLC.
!note Note:
1. If rear cab A9 is in Emergency, brake electronics failure message will be displayed in some loco.
2. If BP not dropping through A9, immediately move A9 to emergency or Open RS.
3. While knorr Loco moved as Dead Loco, if BP dropped, close and open 74 coc in Dead Loco 2 to 3 times and try.

@@2 9  Safety items to be checked in under truck
1. CBC Coupling, operating handle, screw coupling, safety pin.
2. Primary and secondary suspension springs. D-shackle, Horizontal, Vertical and Yaw dampers for any crack, damage or hanging.
3. Traction links, Torque arm fixing bolts, Main Compressors, its securing ropes, fixing bolts, Wheel set Guide and its rod.

@@2 10  Procedure to check ACP apparatus before signing BPC
(AFL Buzzer and Flasher light will not work while Loco is in standstill condition)
1. In E70 Locos AFI shoot up is the indication for ACP.
2. In Knorr CCB2 Locos, EBV display, ‘Train line emergency’ is the indication for ACP

@@2 11  Emergency and FS penalty recovery in CCB 2 locos
2. EBV Display showing ‘Okay to RUN’, BP target 0.00, now only LP to move A9 handle to RUN position to charge BP
Emergency penalty recovery in CCB 2 Loco
1. EBV Display showing ‘Trainline Emergency, put Auto in emergency’, LP to Keep A9 handle in Emergency position till Okay to RUN comes.
Service Penalty recovery in CCB 2 Loco.
1. EBV Display showing ‘Safety Penalty, put Auto in FS’, LP to keep A9 handle in FS for 10 sec.
2. EBV Display shows ‘Okay to RUN, BP target 3.32’, LP to move A9 handle to RUN position to charge BP.

@@2 12  BP continuity and brake tests

@@3 12.1  BP Continuity test (Goods Trains)
- This test to be carried out with coordination of Guard.
- Ensure BP pressure is 5 kg/cm² in loco and 4.8 Kg/cm² in rear Brake van.
- Bring A9 handle to application so as to drop BP pressure to 4 kg/cm².
- Close ‘70 coc’ in E-70 Loco and keep Mode switch in ‘TEST’ in Knorr Loco.
- F 10 04 P1, ‘Working Wrong configuration’ message comes in display.
- Ask guard to open the emergency brake valve until BP drops to ‘0’ and then valve to be closed.
- In case the BV is not the rear most vehicle, open the BP angle cock of rearmost vehicle until BP pressure drops to ‘0’ and then angle cock to be closed.
- The Loco Pilot to observe the BP pressure dropped to '0' and does not commence to rise again.
- Open 70 coc in E-70 Loco and MODE switch to LEAD in Knorr Loco, keep A9 in RUN and charge BP.
- If the BP pressure does not drop to '0' in the loco, check the formation for closing of any BP angle cocks in between the vehicles of the train. If found any, open it.
- After opening of the angle cocks during the course of continuity test, further continuity test must be carried out.

@@3 12.2  BP Continuity test (Coaching Trains)
The Loco Pilot and Guard must confirm for the test by means of communication.
- Charge BP and FP pressure of the train and check that 5.0kg/cm²and 6.0 kg/ cm² is available in BP & FP in the loco.
- Confirm from the Guard that minimum 4.8 kg/ cm² and 5.8 kg/cm² pressure is available in BP and FP Gauge in the trailing SLR.
- Bring A9 handle to application so as to drop BP pressure to 4 kg/cm² and confirm from the Guard that pressure in BP Gauge in the trailing SLR within the range of 3.6 to 4.0 kg/cm².
- Bring A9 handle to RUN position and ensure BP pressure recharges to 5.0 kg/cm² and 4.8 kg/cm² in Loco and trailing SLR respectively.
- Close 70 coc in E-70 Loco and keep MODE switch in TEST in Knorr Loco, (F 10 04 P1, ‘Working Wrong configuration’ message comes in display) and ask guard to open the guard’s emergency brake valve without delay until BP pressure drops to 3.6 kg/ cm² and then Guard Emergency brake valve to be closed.
- In case the SLR is not the trailing most vehicle, open the BP angle cock on the end of the rearmost vehicle until BP pressure drops to 3.6 kg/ cm² and then valve to be closed.
- The Guard must confirm from Loco Pilot that the BP pressure dropped to 3.6 to 4.0 kg/cm² in the Loco.
- If the BP pressure does not drop to 3.6 to 4.0 kg/cm² in the loco, check the formation for closing of any BP angle cocks in between the vehicles of the train. If found any, open it.
- After opening of the angle cocks during the course of continuity test, further continuity test must be carried out.
- Open 70 coc in E-70 Loco and MODE switch to LEAD in Knorr Loco and ensure BP pressure recharges to 5.0 kg/cm² and 4.8 kg/ cm² in Loco and trailing SLR respectively

@@3 12.3  Procedure of Brake Feel Test and Brake Power Test by LPs
!h Brake Feel Test
1. To be done immediately after start from the stat ion where the crew have taken over charge .
2. On attaining the speed of 15 km ph, L P shall drop the BP pressure by 0. 5 kg/cm² through A-9 and release (Press PVEF)
3. The crew should feel the application of brake and speed sh ould d rop to 10 kmph.
!h Brake Power Test.
1. To be done in first Block Section (or on first opportunity as in SR4.32/l(b)
2. Speed of the train should reach 60 to 70kmph in case of coaching train and 40 to 50 Kmph in case of goods train.
3. LP shall drop BP pressure by 1 kg/cm² in Coaching train/loaded goods train and 0.5 kg/cm² in case of Em pty goods train through A-9, released after a pause (Press PVEF).
4. The speed of the train should drop to 30 to 35 Kmph in coaching t rain and 20 to 25 Kmph inGoods t rain, by the lime BP pressure comes back to 5.0 kg/cm².
5. The LP shall observe the effectiveness of Brakes and Brake Power of the train.
6. As the AFI needle comes to '0' normal traction will be resumed.
!h Duties of ALP d u ring Brake Feel Test and Brake Power Test:
1. ALP shall keep a watch and ensure that both the tests are done by LP and will remind him for the same, if required.
2. He will equally participate (carefully watch) in Brake Feel Test and Brake Power Test to assess the effectiveness of the braking of the train.
3. He will note down the locat ion, pressure dropped, number of masts for which A9 was applied and speed of the train at the start and end of the brake feel test and brake power test in his diary.
!note Note:
- Crew shall ensure observation of the signal aspects while performing the BFT and BPT.
- In case of up gradient in first block section, suitable guidelines will be issued by the division as per terrain condition to avoid stalling.

@@2 13  Important points for MU operation
1. Fault message of both loco will appear on master loco display unit with loco number. Immediately note down the message with loco number and time in rough journal.
2. Acknowledge the P1 fault by Throttle -0 and press BPFA.
3. Observe the FLG Node of both loco, then trouble shoot as per fault number on the basis of FLG Node.
4. Slave Node shows 0 during TRAIN BUS COMMUNICATION failed.
5. First keep BL Key from D to OFF position in leading loco, Information message 001 (loco in self hold mode) shows on DDU in leading loco.
6. Then Proceed to trailing CAB of Slave Loco and CE OFF as per procedure. After CE OFF, again CE ON, wait for self-tests and FLG NODE 504. After completion of self- test and try to energise both loco from slave loco, if successful, clear block section.
7. If fault not rectified, then rear loco is to be as made Trail dead and work the train with leading loco, if load permits.
8. CE ON/OFF possible from any CAB of both locos but driving possible from Free End CAB only (During troubleshooting switch OFF/ON only From Free end cab).
9. Switch OFF MCE of Slave loco not possible from master loco immediately

@@2 14  DPWCS (Distributed Power Wireless Control System) Operating Instructions
To Configure as Lead
1. Both locomotives should be connected in same consist and Train should be in stand still condition and Loco Brake Should be in applied condition.
2. Switch on DPWCS MCB, Enter Driver ID, Password & Train Number
3. Select Lead, Working Cab direction (Cab 1 Front or Cab2 Front), Total Number of remote Locomotives, Remote locomotive Numbers, and Wagon Type and no of Wagons.
4. Press Initiate Grouping and follow the instructions on display for BP continuity Check.
To Configure as Remote
1. Coc Position: Coc 47: Closed, Coc 74: Open, Coc 136: Open, Coc 70: Open.
2. Release SA9 in both cabs
3. Put the BL Key in Drive Mode and wait for 504 Node. After 504 put the BL key in off position and take out the Key (Node should be 612).
4. Switch on DPWCS MCB, Enter Loco Pilot ID, Password and Train Number.
5. Select remote, Cab movement direction (Cab 1 Front or Cab2 Front) and Lead LocoNumber.
6. Press Initiate Grouping.
Neutral Section Negotiation
1. Press the NSN Button provided on the Driver Display Unit on crossing 500Meter
!note Warning Board.
2. Open the VCB at open Board & Close the VCB at Close Board.
3. The remote locomotive will open its VCB before the open Board and Close after the VCB close board automatically. Relevant message will be displayed on DDU for loco.
Ungrouping
1. Train must be in standstill condition and loco brake should be applied.
2. In Lead locomotive, Press “Ungroup DPWCS” and press “Yes” to confirm the Ungroup request.
3. Both locos get ungrouped and login screen will be displayed on DDU in both locos.

@@2 Trail Dead and Trailing Mode working procedure
PROCEDURE TO WORK WITH SINGLE LOCO IF ANYONE OF WAG9 MU FAILS (If road and load permits)
CASE: 1 SLAVE (REAR) LOCO FAILURE
- For any reason, If Slave Loco failed, for e.g. Fatal error
- Due to Pressure/Temperature sensors (Transducers) defect
- More than One Identical Subsystems isolated, like
- SR1 and SR2 both isolated
- Any Two BUR isolated
- LP can work the Train with Single Loco after making Slave Loco Dead, If load and Road permits
- It is called Trail Dead
- If Slave (trailing) loco trips with any fault then fault massage with Slave loco number will be displayed in Master loco
- LP has to note down the fault and acknowledge by pressing BPFA for getting the corresponding node information about the nature of failure in rear loco.
- If node information of Slave loco is similar as Master loco proceed with normal operation.
- If Slave node Shows 504 in DDU message, then clear section within 10 minutes if load permits otherwise MCE off automatically with BP Pressure drops.
- Slave Node shows 0 during TRAIN BUS COMMUNICATION failed.
- If node information of Slave loco is not similar as Master loco, remove BL key (D to OFF), proceed to Slave loco, by insert BL Key, move OFF to D position make the node 504 then turn D to OFF (Self Hold Mode )
- Then proceed to Master loco, ensure both loco node is 504 and energize both locos.
- If unable to make the Slave loco node 504, then make the Slave loco TRAIL DEAD.
- If load and road permits work from Master loco upto destination. Otherwise contact TLC
Procedure during Failure of Slave loco in MU with CCB2 (Trail DEAD)
- Switch OFF CE of both Locos.
- Switch OFF MCB 112.1 in SB-2 panel of Slave loco.
- Close isolating cock 74 (Emergency vigilance isolating cock).
- Close isolating cock 136 (Feed pipe isolating cock).
- Cock 47 (Dead engine cock) remains closed.
Procedure during Failure of Slave loco in MU Operation with E70 Brake System
- Close 70, 74 and 136 cocs. 47 coc remains in closed condition.
- Close Direct Brake Equalizing (BC) Angle COC in between locos to avoid BC pilot pressure leakage from dead loco.
- Manually release the parking brake. (Ensure loco brake should release).
- Now switch “ON” CE of leading loco and energize the loco. Now train can work as single loco.
- With SA9 loco brakes will be applied only in Master loco, Slave loco brakes will be applied with A9 only.
CASE: 2 MASTER (LEADING) LOCO FAILURE
- For any reason if Leading Loco Traction failed,
- Like Fatal error, both Bogie isolation.
- Two BUR isolation
- And unable to Troubleshoot
- LP can work from Leading Loco ( Using leading Loco Battery and CE), With Slave Loco Traction Power
- That is called Trailing Mode
- LP can clear section or upto Battery voltage comes to 92volt
- Trailing mode means that on the master loco in a MU No TE/BE is available, if both bogies are electrically isolated (154 in I+II).
- The Loco Pilot’s cab of the master loco still controls the slave loco.
Trailing mode
- The status of the master locomotive is as follows:
- VCB open, Pantograph is lowered.
- Train bus is working correctly.
- Pneumatic brake system is working correctly.
- Brakes are controlled from the master locomotive.
- The TE/BE meters show the values for the slave locomotive.
- All equipment on the master locomotive are supplied by the battery only.
- Stop and secure the loco, Open VCB, Lower Pantograph and Switch OFF MCE of Leading Loco.
- Keep 154 switch of Leading Loco on “I+II” (Both bogies are electrically isolated).
- Switch ON MCE as per procedure, Master loco NODE information shows 504 (not change) and Slave locos NODE will change as per sequence.
- Energize the Slave loco from Master loco working cab.
- LSDJ remains glowing in Master loco.
- Release the parking brake by pressing BPPB.
- Put reverser in desired direction and take TE, ensure FLG 596.
- Now the tractive effort of trailing loco will be displayed on leading loco TE/BE meter and also on simulation screen.
- Pneumatic Brakes controlling from leading Loco.
- Release SA-9 and resume traction

@@2 1.2  Three phase 415 V auxiliary motors
!h Normal Load distribution during all 3 BURs in service.

@@2 1.3  Load sharing of Auxiliary Converters (BUR) if any one is isolated
Auxiliary Converter (BUR) Isolated. Generally all the 3 Auxiliary converters are loaded equally, but in case of any one Auxiliary converter fails, the other Two Auxiliary converters shares its load. While On Run, if anyone BUR isolated by CE, inform and get permission from TLC and work with other Two BURs. Load sharing after isolation of any one Auxiliary converter (BUR) as follows.

@@2 2.1  Instructions to LPs/ALPs (regarding DDS messages)
While communicating loco troubles to TLC/shed/CLI, Inform correct fault number, front end message and DDS message (back ground message) recorded at that time of loco trouble. Make remarks in the log book / Rough journal book. Reading DDS (Back ground Message) in MICAS and C-DAC. PRESS THE MAIN MENU and SELECT PROCESS INFORMATION, PRESS ENTER SELECT COMMISSIONING DATA and PRESS ENTER SELECT DIAGNOSTIC DATA SET and PRESS ENTER
‘+000’ is the latest Message and -001, -002….. Are previous messages. Note it in Rough Journal Book and inform TLC/Shed alongwith Front End Fault message. Note: In some C-DAC locos, Sub-System Isolation is coming with Number. For e.g: F 01 00 P0 is for SS 01 Main Power isolated, F 02 00 P0 is for SS 02 Bogie 1 Isolated and so on…for SS03, SS04, SS06, SS07, SS08, SS13, SS14, SS17, SS18, and SS19 Isolation.

@@2 2.4  Normal and Fault NODE numbers (MICAS)
!h NODE Number sequence during Energisation
1. MCB 112.1 in SB2 panel ON (ensure all MCBs normal in HB1, HB2, SB1 and SB2 panels), BL key OFF to D, after self-test, CE ON _____________________________FLG 504 comes.
2. Press ZPT, Pantograph raises, U meter shows OHE voltage _____________FLG 550 comes.
3. Press BLDJ, LSDJ extinguished ____________________________________FLG 570 comes.
4. After MR pressure reaches more than 6.4kg/sq.cm, move MPJ to F/R_____FLG 590 comes.
5. From 570 to 590, Converter contactors are closed, DC link voltage ready.
6. Move TE/BE handle towards Traction/braking side ____________________FLG 596 (after releasing Parking brake) comes.
7. At 592 Harmonic Filter contactors closed, HF comes to service, at 596 Drive converter ready.

@@2 4.1  Instructions for VCU Reset
- In case of Transient failures, to bring back the isolated subsystem to service,
- Normally we will switch OFF/ON CE through BL key. With this procedure there is loss of punctuality due to BP drop in the whole formation.
- To avoid such cases, we can use “MCE Reset/VCU Reset” Push button which is provided in SB1 panel to de-energise 218 (Contactor for CE)
- To reset CE through VCU Reset Switch, trip VCB and lower Pantograph, ensure Node 504
- Press VCU Reset Push Button Switch for 5 to 8 seconds(BP will not drop) and 20 seconds in case of TCN VCU (CDAC) identified by FLG1: 111/112 or higher version Software.
- If LSVW flickers, VCD to be acknowledge through PVCD, otherwise VCD will act.
- Once VCU Reset Switch is pressed, ensure Display gets reboot and node numbers start progressing from FLG 0 – 504. Then raise Pantograph and close VCB.
- VCU Reset Switch can be operated during coasting of Locomotive.
- In case of BHEL/ABB IGBT make SR fitted Locos (other than ELS/ED locos), VCU Reset Switch should be operated only after Stopping the train so as to avoid further problems. (TLTE )
- Normal Pneumatic Braking Operation through A9 and SA9 can be carried out (Brake Electronics remains in service). It ensures safe working of the train under all circumstances.
- VCU Reset Switch can be used only for transient fault. If the fault is permanent, first isolate the subsystem through MCB or Cut-Out Switch and then VCU Reset Switch operation can be done. VCU reset should NOT be used,
- If the trouble is with SS10 (Brake Electronics), Switch OFF/ON CE through BL key only.
- If any MCB tripped (which are all controlled by 218 contactor) in SB1/SB2 panel. Stop train, Switch OFF CE, reset MCB and Switch ON CE through BL key.
!note Note: While Switch OFF/ON CE through BL key, Stop train, O Open VCB, Lower pantograph, ensure Node 504, Apply A9 to Emergency to avoid MR dropping, then move BL key D-OFF-C-OFF.
!h VCU RESET PUSH BUTTON IN SB1 PANEL
KEEP MPJ in ‘0’ KEEPK KK

@@2 2. Loco Preparation and Inspection | ch=2
2. Loco Preparation and Inspection: Before commencing journey, do an thorough checking of the locomotive and the machine room.
In particular, check the following items:

@@3 2.1  Cab Front Side
1. Ensure loco is on the rail and under the OHE.
2. Ensure both sides MRE, BCE, BP, FP hoses are connected properly and their angle COC is as per requirement. Also ensure hose pipes are not hanging.
3. Ensure rail guard, cattle guard, has no damages.
4. Ensure CBC having no abnormalities and locking pin is available and provided with operating handle.
5. Ensure UIC connector sockets are covered and both end Hotel Load IV coupler is closed and locked.
6. Ensure flasher light, marker lights, headlights are glowing properly and their glasses are clean.
7. Ensure both look out glasses are clean and wipers are wiping properly.

@@3 2.2  Machine Room No 1 Side (CAB 1 TO CAB 2)
1. Ensure all sand boxes are filled with dry fine sand and sanders are working properly.
2. Ensure additional angle COC of MRE and DBE is opened.
3. Ensure air dryer unit is in service i.e., D-in open, D- out open and D-off closed.
4. Ensure that primary vertical dampers, secondary vertical dampers, yaw dampers and horizontal dampers are having no abnormalities like, crack etc.
5. Wheel set guide rods are fitted properly and its bolts are tightened.
6. All brake rigging, brake block, brake shoe, Brake pull rod, assembly hanger arrangement are normal.
7. Wheels are not in skidded condition.
8. Earth return current cable is connected properly in wheel numbers 1&7.
9. Parking brake cylinders (WAP 5 & WAG 9) are provided with manual release handle.
10. Traction link is fitted properly and their all bolts are tightened and Ensure Traction link Safety rope is intact.
11. Oil level in MCP 1 is above minimum mark and also ensure its Base fixing bolt are tightened and safety rope is intact.
12. BIC -1 and ULV-1 COC are in open condition.
13. Battery box-1 cover is locked.
14. Check the Transformer oil pump-1 for any abnormality.
15. Safety chain is intact.
16. BP and FP additional COC are in open condition.

@@3 2.3  Machine Room No 2 Side (CAB 2 TO CAB 1)
1. Ensure that all sand boxes are filled with dry fine sand and its cover in closed condition and sanders are working properly.
2. Ensure that additional angle COC of MRE and DBE are opened.
3. Ensure that primary vertical dampers, secondary vertical dampers, yaw dampers and horizontal dampers are having no abnormalities like, crack etc.
4. Wheel set guide rods are fitted properly and its bolts are tightened.
5. All brake rigging, brake block, brake shoe, brake pull rod, assembly hanger arrangement are normal.
6. Wheels are not skidded.
7. Earth return current cable is connected properly in wheel number 6&12
8. Parking brake cylinders (WAP 5 & WAG 9) are provided with manual release handle.
9. Traction link is fitted properly and their all bolts are tightened and Ensure Traction link Safety rope is intact.
10. Oil level in MCP 2 is above minimum mark, also ensure its Base fixing bolts are tightened and safety rope is intact.
11. BIC-2 COC and ULV-2 COC are in open condition.
12. Battery box-2 cover is locked, MCB 112 cover is intact.
13. Check the transformer oil pump-2 for any abnormality.
14. Safety chain is intact. 15.BP and FP additional COC are in open condition.

@@3 2.12  Machine Room
1. Check both SR - 1 and 2 oil (Coolant) levels between Minimum and Maximum mark and should not have any oil leakage near the converter. Silica gel normally in blue colour which absorbs the moisture, if moisture content increases the colour of the silica gel will turn to pink.
2. In some Locos the Silica gel is in golden colour which absorbs the moisture,if moisture content increases the colour of the silica gel will turn to Green.
3. Control circuit MCBs in SB-1, SB-2 panels & Auxiliary Motors MCBs in HB-1 and HB-2 Panels are normal.
4. Switches provided in SB-1 are at normal position.
5. Oil (Coolant) levels of converter - 1 & 2 and transformer - 1 & 2 expansion tanks are above minimum mark.
6. Ensure doors of all power equipment units are closed.

@@2 3. Loco Online Movement | ch=3
Loco on line movement

@@3 3.1  Energisation of Loco
1. Check the machine room and cubicle HB1& 2, SB1& 2, Pneumatic panel and ensure that all circuit breakers, rotating switches and cocs in pneumatic panel are in normal position.
2. Ensure that IG-38 key is in Horizontal position in E70 & Vertical position in KNORR Brake locos.
3. Ensure that pantograph selector switch is at Auto position. CAB SETUP: E70 KNORR BRAKE WORKING CAB SA-9 in Applied. Insert A-9 in Neutral position and move it to RUN position. MPJ in Neutral
SA-9 in Applied. A-9 in RUN position. MODE switch in LEAD MPJ in Neutral NON WORKING CAB
SA-9 in Release. Remove A-9 in Neutral position. MPJ in Neutral.
SA-9 in Release. A-9 handle locked in FS position. MODE switch in TRAIL MPJ in Neutral.
4. Switch on ZLFW, glowing of marker lights ensures Battery Breaker MCB 112 (BA box no.2) is in ON position.
5. Switch on Control circuit breaker No.112.1 and MCB 110 in SB-2 Panel.
6. Insert BL key in OFF position and turn it to D position.
7. These Following actions to be observed a. Display screen Boot up. b. Speedometer light glows & Needle Deviates, c. MCPA starts Automatically (if pressure is less), d. UBA shows battery voltage, e. LSDJ glows.
8. The message “Loco is in configuration please wait” will appear on the screen and self-test takes place for 10 seconds.
9. Bogies-1 and 2 meter will deviate both in TE/BE mode and come to 0. All pilot lamps glow and extinguish and display shows FLG504.
10. All above, indicates that Control Electronics is ON.
11. After FLG504 appeared on the screen, now press ZPT downwards (UP position) for raising pantograph and observer the following actions
a. Physically ensure the rear pantograph raises and touches the OHE properly. b. Node FLG550 appears in display. c. U-meter will deviate and indicates the OHE voltage.
12. Now press BLDJ downwards for closing VCB and observer the following actions a. VCB closed & LSDJ is extinguished. b. Node FLG570 appears in display. c. All Auxiliaries started working.
13. Now loco is energised and all the auxiliary motors & CHBA starts automatically.
14. Ensure BLCP is in AUTO position, un-loader valves pick up and MCP contactors closed after 4 seconds.
15. Wait till MR pressure builds up to 6.4 kg/cm 2 , and move reverser handle to Forward / Reverse. Convertor pre-charging contactors and main contactors closed, DC link Charged and FLG 590 appears.
16. If the reverser handle is moved without confirming the MR pressure i.e. above 6.4 kg/cm 2 , then P-1 fault message F10 02 P1 will appear.
17. Ensure BC gauge reads 3.5 Kg/cm 2 , MR 8-10 Kg/cm 2 and BP 5 Kg/cm 2
18. Release the parking brakes (press BPPB and ensure red illumination goes off on BPPB and PB gauge reads 6 kg/cm 2 in PB provided loco). If throttle is operated without releasing parking brakes priority -1 message appears,
19. Ensure AFI is at Zero, Keep display in simulation mode.
20. Now move throttle in TE direction, At FLG592 Harmonic filter contactors closed, FLG596 appears.
21. Information message will appear on the screen if throttle is operated before node information FLG596.
22. TE /BE meter deviates.
23. Check the loco brake power, Loco should not move upto 150KN in WAP 7 & WAG 9 and upto 100KN in WAP 5.
24. Release loco brake.
25. Now loco can be moved.

@@3 3.2  Loco Brake Test
1. Apply SA-9
2. Ensure that brake cylinders gauge reads 3.5 kg / cm 2 pressure.
3. Release parking brakes and ensure A9 handle in RUN position.
4. Operate MPJ to forward.
5. Set simulation screen.
6. Move throttle to traction side and increase TE up to 150 KN for WAP 7/WAG 9 and 100 KN for WAP 5 locos.
7. Loco should not move.

@@3 3.3  Continuity Test
1. This test to be carried out in co-ordination with guard.
2. The LP and Guard shall start the test through walkie-talkie communication.
3. During the test, a fault message “Wrong Configuration” F10 04 P1 appears, while closing 70 COC, (keep mode switch in TEST in KNORR Loco)
4. Press BPFA after completion of test.
Continuity test for goods trains:
1. Ensure BP pressure is charged in the loco to 5 kg/cm² and 4.8 Kg/cm² in Guard van.
2. Move A9 handle to application position without pause so as to drop BP pressure to 4 kg/cm² and close 70 COC. Keep Mode switch in TEST in case of KNORR brake loco.
3. Now inform guard to open the guard’s brake valve until all the air pressure is exhausted to 0 kg/cm 2 , then valve to be closed.
4. In case the BV is not the rear most vehicle, open the BP angle cock on the rear end of the last vehicle until all air is exhausted and angle cock to be closed.
5. The LP to observe in the leading cab BP pressure dropped to '0' kg/cm 2 and does not commence to rise again.
6. If the BP pressure does not fall to 0 in the loco, check the formation for any fault or abnormality.
7. Normalize the A9 and 70 COC / Mode switch and ensure BP pressure is charged to 5 kg/cm² (4.8 Kg/cm² for upto 56BOXN and 4.7Kg/cm 2 for more than 56BOXN) in Guard van.
8. After correction of any fault during the course of continuity test, further continuity test must be carried out.
Continuity test for pass/express trains.
1. Charge BP and FP pressure of the train and ensure that 5.0 +/- 0.1% kg/cm² and 6.0 +/- 0.1% kg/cm 2 pressure is available in BP & FP Gauge respectively in the working cab.
2. Confirm from the Guard that minimum 4.8 kg/cm 2 and 5.8 kg/cm 2 pressure is available in BP & FP Gauge respectively in the rear SLR.
3. Move A9 handle to application so as to drop BP pressure to 4 kg/cm² and the Guard to confirm that pressure dropped in BP gauge in the rear SLR within the range of 3.6 to 4.0 kg/cm².
4. Move A9 handle to release position and ensure BP pressure recharges to 5.0 +/- 0.1% kg/cm² and 4.8 kg/cm 2 in loco and rear SLR respectively.
5. In E70 Loco close 70 COC, in KNORR Loco Keep Mode switch in TEST and inform guard to open the guard’s emergency brake valve without delay until BP pressure drops to 3.6 kg/cm 2 and then Guard Emergency brake valve to be closed.
6. In case the SLR is not the rear most vehicle, open the BP angle cock on the rear end of the last vehicle until BP pressure drops to 3.6 kg/cm 2 and then valve to be closed.
7. The Guard must confirm from LP that the BP pressure dropped to 3.6 to 4.0 kg/cm 2 in the loco.
8. If the BP pressure does not drop to3.6 to 4.0 kg/cm 2 in the loco, check the formation for closing of any BP angle cocks in between the vehicles of the train. If found any, open it.
9. After opening of the angle cocks during the course of continuity test, further continuity test must be carried out. 10.In E70 Loco open 70 COC, in KNORR Loco keep mode switch in LEAD and ensure BP pressure recharges to 5.0 +/- 0.1% kg/cm²and 4.8 kg/cm 2 in loco and rear SLR respectively and press BPFA.

@@3 3.4  Driving
1. Ensure BP 5 kg/cm 2 .
2. Check AFI is stable and showing present leakage level. Set the red needle coincide with white needle.
3. Release the parking brake if applied.
4. Move the throttle to TE side, Ensure Node FLG596 and KN in display.
5. Release SA9.
6. Use sanders to increase adhesion and to avoid wheel slipping.
7. Bogies 1 and 2 meters deviate in TE side and loco starts moving.

@@3 3.5  Braking
1. Bring the TE/BE handle to 0 and move towards braking side as per requirement to control the train.
2. Maximum brake force is 260 KN in WAG-9/WAP-7, 160 KN in WAP-5 and 325KN in WAG-9H. Regenerative braking is possible up to 0 KMPH.
3. Use the sanders if required while stopping in up gradient.
4. Move A9 from RUN to initial application. If more brake force is required move A9 handle to further application positions.
5. After train stops, apply SA9, ensure BC pressure.
6. During shunt movement use only A9/SA9 and avoid using RB.
!note Note: - Train brakes can be applied along with regenerating braking but loco brake cannot be applied simultaneously with regenerative braking.

@@3 3.6  Automatic Vigilance Control System
VCD is a safety device provided for ensuring the alertness of the crew. It is active only if speed of train is more than 1.5 KMPH. LP to acknowledge VCD by any one of the following methods.
1. Press VCD Foot pedal switch,
2. Press BPVG,
3. Operate throttle in TE/BE with a movement of more than 3% or
4. Operate sander pedal switch.
If VCD is acknowledged through any one of the above method the Loco Pilot is considered vigilant and the 60 seconds cycle is reset. If no acknowledgement within 60 seconds, then buzzer sounds and LSVW glows for next 8 seconds. Within this 8 seconds LP to acknowledge VCD by either pressing vigilance foot pedal switch or BPVG. If not acknowledged, VCD penalty brakes will apply and BP pressure drops to 2.5 – 3.0 kg/cm 2 . F10 03 P1 fault message will appear in display. TE/BE will become 0.
Dead man’s mode: If VCD foot switch/BPVG remains pressed for more than 60 second, then the system will go automatically in dead man mode, and buzzer will start, if not released with next 8 second, VCD penalty will be applied. The BP drops 2.5 to 3.0 kg/cm 2 .
Resetting of VCD Penalty Brakes:
1. Bring throttle to 0.
2. Apply A9 to emergency (To avoid MR dropping) and place MPJ in Neutral.
2. Wait for 120 seconds in E70 Loco (32 second in KNORR Loco CCB2)
3. Press BPVR to reset VCD, LSVW extinguishes and buzzer stops.
4. Press and release VCD foot pedal switch.
5. Press BPFA for acknowledging fault message.
6. Bring A9 handle to RUN position to recharge BP pressure.
7. Resume normal traction.
Isolation of VCD: If Loco pilot is acknowledging VCD properly as per procedure, but still VCD penalty brake is taking place, isolate VCD by keeping 237.1 (SB1) in 0 position. Make remark in the log book and inform TLC and proceed vigilantly.

@@3 3.7  Constant Speed Controller
This system enables the train to maintain a constant speed automatically and can be activated at any speed above 5 KMPH by pressing push button BPCS. The CE controls the constant speed by giving tractive or braking effort to the TM as per requirement irrespective of position of throttle. If BPCS pressed for more than 5 seconds, BPCS struck up message will appear and become non-functional. CSC is activated only when,
1. Speed is more than 5 KMPH
2. Throttle to be kept in TE or BE side.
3. A9/SA9 is not in applied condition.
4. When constant speed is activated self-illuminated BPCS green lamp glows. The action of CSC is cancelled by following conditions.
1. By Moving throttle.
2. Pressing BPCS while CSC is active, lamp extinguishes.
3. Dropping BP pressure by 0.25 kg/cm 2 or above (BP gauge showing 4.75 kg/cm 2 ).
4. By applying SA9, BC pressure reads more than 0.6 kg/cm 2 .
!note Note:
1. To minimize oscillation, the CSC should be set when train is accelerating slowly.
2. CSC will maintain the speed only up to max TE / BE capacity. So, in undulating section the speed may vary +/- 10 Kmph to the preset value. So in undulated sections BPCS should be pressed at the speed with margin of 10 KMPH. Also in some sections where special driving technique is required, CSC may stall the train. So, in such sections, instead of using CSC, LP should use his best driving technique by operation of throttle.
3. Before cancelling CSC ensure the needle position of TE/BE meter and throttle are not just opposite. If so, first bring the throttle 0 and then cancel CSC.

@@3 3.8  Emergency Stop Push Button
Red colour push button is provided on panel A for emergency use. In normal condition it remains projected out. In case of emergency LP/ALP can press this button for stopping train. If the button is pressed, these following actions are initiated.
1. VCB opens.
2. TE/BE meter comes to 0,
3. Pantograph lowers.
4. BP drops to 0 kg/cm 2
- Train/Loco brakes applied.
5. LSFI flickers /BPFA glows with message F10 08 P1 “Emergency shutdown on the loco” displays.
6. Flasher light glows automatically.
Resetting:
1. Bring throttle to 0.
2. Bring MPJ to Neutral.
3. Rotate Emergency stop push button slightly in clockwise direction or as per arrow indication on it, the button will come back to normal position.
4. Press BPFA.
5. Energise the loco and work the train.

@@3 3.9  Passing Neutral Section
Before approaching neutral section:
1. Ensure that MR pressure is between 8.5 to 10.0 kg/cm 2
2. Bring throttle to 0 gradually before 250 M warning board.
3. At DJ open board, LP to open VCB, LSDJ glows, but U meter shows OHE supply.
4. At actual neutral section location, U meter needle drops and rise again showing OHE voltage.
5. At DJ close board, close VCB (Ensure FLG590) and resume normal traction.
7. ALP should check the following, if there is no stop signal / level crossing gate is approaching immediately. a) Check for any smoke, smell and abnormal sound in the machine room equipment and auxiliaries. b) Ensure MCBs in SB1, SB2, and HB1 & HB2 are in normal position. c) Check for any abnormality in TFR oils, SR oil/coolant levels, silica gel condition and water entry into the machine room. d) Ensure RS gauge reads above 6 kg/cm 2 . Check the rear cab for any abnormality. e) If any abnormality noticed, it should be attended as per TSD and also make entry in the log book and inform to TLC. Passing neutral section (MU)
- While passing neutral section follow the procedure mentioned for SU.
- Close VCB after passing one OHE mast after DJ close board.
- The command from the master locomotive to open/close VCB also controls the VCB of the slave loco. (Ensure Node numbers of BOTH LOCO shows FLG590)
- During MU operations for Closing VCB in both Loco, Press BLDJ from the MASTER Loco. Master Loco VCB Closes immediately and Slave Loco VCB will close after a delay of 0.5 seconds.

@@3 3.10  Cab Changing
Single Unit
1. Apply SA-9 (Ensure FLG590)
2. Move MPJ to Neutral. (Ensure FLG570)
3. Open VCB. (Ensure FLG550)
4. Lower pantograph. (Ensure FLG504)
5. Move A9 handle to Emergency position & lift the lock and then move it to Neutral position and remove A9 handle in E70 Loco. (In KNORR loco keep A9 in FS and Lock and Keep Mode switch in TRAIL)
6. Operate BL key from D to off (self-hold mode) and remove it. (CE will remain ON for 10 minutes) and go to other cab.
7. Apply SA9 (In BOTH E70 & KNORR Brake).
8. Insert BL key in 0 and Move to D (Ensure FLG504).
9. Energize the loco.
10. Insert A9 handle in Neutral and bring it to ‘Run’ position in E70 loco (In KNORR loco keep mode switch in LEAD & then remove the Locking pin and Move A9 Handle to RUN position.)
11. Release SA9 in rear cab (In both E70 & KNORR Brake).
!note Note: After moving BL key from D to OFF & removed, SA9 will not work and A9 will work in emergency position only (Hence apply SA9 ensure BC pressure 3.5kg/cm 2 before initiating cab change). After removing the BL key, the CE remains active for 10 minutes. Cab changing should be done within 10mins. If BL key is moved from D to OFF without opening VCB and lowering pantograph, loco will shutdown as Emergency shutdown. During normal driving if BL key is moved to OFF, emergency brakes get applied as emergency shutdown will initiated on loco.
Multiple units
1. Apply SA-9 (Ensure FLG590).
2. Move MPJ to Neutral (Ensure FLG570).
3. Open VCB (Ensure FLG550).
4. Lower pantograph (Ensure FLG504).
5. Move A9 handle to Emergency position and lift the lock, then move it to Neutral position and then remove handle in E70. In KNORR Loco Lock A9 in FS position and keep the Mode switch in TRAIL.
6. Operate BL key from D to off and remove it. (CE will remain ON for 10).
7. Release SA-9.
8. Close 136 COC (now Slave Loco) and go to master loco leading cab.
9. Open 136 COC in master loco.
10. Insert BL key in OFF position and Move to D in master loco leading cab (Ensure node showing FLG504 for both locos).
11. Raise Pantograph (Ensure node changes to FLG550 for both locos).
12. Close VCB (Ensure node changes to FLG570 for both locos).
!note Note: Cab changing of SU and MU loco should be done within 10 minutes, otherwise the CE will switched off automatically.

@@3 3.11  Auto Brake Test
When A9 handle in RUN position BP should charge against permitted leakage in prescribed time limit. If MR / BP not charging within normal time against permissive leakage in the formation, this test may be conducted in presence of CLI and SSE.
- Allow the MR pressure to build up to maximum stipulated limit.
- Close the BP angle cock and charge the BP to 5 kg/cm 2 by A9 run position.
- Couple 7.5mm dia leak hole test coupler to the BP hose pipe of locomotive. Open BP angle cock. BP Pressure should not drop below 4 kg/cm 2 within 60 Seconds. This test has to be conducted with both compressors working.

@@3 3.12  Examination of Loco At Crew Changing Point
By incoming loco pilot:
1. Incoming loco pilot after stopping the train, apply SA9, operate sander pedal PSA with MPJ in F and R.
2. Switch ON head light and flasher light.
3. Mention all the important repairs and abnormality observed on run, in the log book and also inform to the out going loco pilot.
4. In case of abnormal detentions enroute, it should be informed to TLC on duty.
5. Ensure sand boxes are filled with dry sand.
6. Wind shield washing unit tanks are filled with water & wipers are wipping.
Out going loco pilot:
1. Out going loco pilot should check the working of head light, flasher light, air flow indicator and temperature of axle boxes. Switch off head light and flasher light after testing.
2. Check OCR 78 relay.
3. Check for primary vertical dampers, Yaw dampers, Horizontal dampers, wheel set guide rod, traction motor mounting plate and its bolts, traction links, its bolts & safety rope and brake riggings.
4. Check for compressor mounting bolt with safety rope.
5. Check for Earthing bushes, speedometer gear boxes and bogie frame.
6. Check for any oil leakage from transformer oil tank and drain plug.
7. Check the condition of air bellows, battery boxes.
8. Check the cattle guard, rail guard, buffers, and CBC fittings.
9. Ensure that front coupling is secured properly and locking pin is properly inserted.
10. Check the adequate fall of sand on rail from all sand pipes.
11. Check the air dryer COC position and ensure it is in service.
12. Check the BIC 1 and 2 are in open condition.
13. Check the loco log book and enquire the particulars regarding any defective apparatus or unusual occurrence from incoming Loco Pilot.
14. Check the BP, MR levels are normal.
15. If any abnormality found while inspection it should be informed to the section TLC.
By Asst loco pilot:
1. Check MCP 1 and MCP 2 oil level after switching off BLCP.
2. Check compressor and its mounting bolt with safety rope.
3. Check the bogie frame, primary vertical dampers, Yaw dampers, Horizontal dampers, wheel set guide rod, traction motor mounting plate and its bolts, traction links, its bolts & its safety rope and brake riggings.
4. Check Earthing bushes and speedometer gear boxes.
5. Check for any oil leakage from transformer oil tank and drain plug,
6. Check the condition of air bellows, battery boxes.
7. Check the cattle guard, rail guard, buffers, and CBC fittings.
8. Ensure that front coupling is secured properly, Operating handle and locking pin is properly inserted.
9. Check OCR 78 relay.
10. Check the transformer oil level and converter oil/coolant level in machine room 1 and 2.
11. Check MCBs in SB1, HB1, SB2 & HB2 panel and rotating switches.
12. Check cut out cocs, C3W Distributor value in service in pneumatic panel.
13. Check the fire extinguishers & its due date.
14. Check the availability of dry sand in sand boxes and spare sand bags.
15. Drain out the moisture from MR1, MR2, AR and CPA / RS drain coc.
16. If any abnormality found while inspection it should be informed to the section TLC.

@@3 3.13  Quick Examination During Short Stoppage At En-Route
1. Drain out moisture from main reservoirs and auxiliary reservoir.
2. Check the temperature of axle boxes.
3. Check the condition of bogie frame, helical springs, dampers, wheel set guide rods, traction link & its bolt and safety rope, torque arm bolts, sand pipes, brake riggings, speedometer gear case fittings.
4. Check for any oil leakage from gear case and condition of air bellows for any damage.
5. Check the proper working of MCP, its foundation bolts and safety ropes are intact and the condition of battery box covers.
6. Check the both end cattle guard, rail guard and buffer for any abnormality.
7. Ensure front end screw coupling is secured in the hook and rear screw coupling is tight and safety pin is intact.
8. In case of express /passenger trains ensure U clamp is intact.

@@3 3.14  Stabling of Loco
1. Bring throttle to 0.
2. Stop the loco by applying brakes.
3. MPJ-Neutral, Open VCB and lower the pantograph.
4. Remove A9 handle and switch off CE.
5. Ensure parking brakes applied (If available in WAG9).
6. Switch off cab lights & corridor lights if glowing.
7. Switch off the MCB 112.1 in SB2 panel.
8. Apply hand brake and place wooden wedges for WAP7.
9. Close the doors and shutters on both sides.
10. Hand over keys and log book to authorised person.
3.15 Assistant loco pilot should apply emergency brakes by opening of RS valve, in case Loco pilot is not vigilant or not reacting according to the signal aspect.

@@2 4. Operation of Rotating Switches and Other Equipments | ch=4
Operation of Rotating switches and other equipments.

@@3 4.1  Switching ON Control Electronics
Driving mode (BL key - off to D) This mode is for driving the loco. After moving BL key OFF to D, These Following actions to be observed: a. Display screen Boot up. b. Speedometer light glows & Needle Deviates, c. MCPA starts automatically (if pressure is less), d. UBA shows battery voltage, e. LSDJ glows. The message “Loco is in configuration please wait” will appear on the screen and self-test takes place for 10 seconds. Bogies-1 and 2 meter will deviate both in TE/BE mode and come to 0. All pilot lamps glow and extinguish and display shows FLG504 & as per the sequence Loco can be energised.

@@3 4.2  Switching OFF Control Electronics
1. Bring throttle to 0 (FLG590), Apply A9 emergency and apply SA9.
2. Bring MPJ to 0. (FLG570)
3. Open VCB. (FLG550)
4. Lower the Pantograph. (FLG504)
5. Move the BL key from D to OFF and wait for 2 seconds. Parking brakes will apply.
6. Then move BL key from OFF to C wait for 10 seconds. Display screen blank.
7. Move BL key from C to OFF.
8. Now control circuits and control electronics switched off completely .

@@3 4.3  Operation of Auxiliary Compressor
The auxiliary compressor is used initially to create pressure for raising the pantograph and to close VCB. Whenever BL key is in D or C and self hold mode, auxiliary compressor starts working automatically. RS pressure gauge,
drain coc and safety valve are provided in pneumatic panel. Normally drain coc handle should be parallel to pipe line (i.e. closed).
MCPA is protected by MCB 48.1 (provided in SB-2 pane). Contactor 48.2 closes when pressure drops to 7kg/cm 2 and opens when pressure reaches 8kg/cm 2 . The working of MCPA is independent of CE.

@@3 4.4  Operation of Pantograph
Two pantographs are provided on either end of loco. Pantograph /VCB control MCB is 127.12 (provided in SB-1 panel) Pantograph can be raised in driving and cooling mode. ZPT switch controls raising and lowering of pantograph. It has 3 positions UP, 0 and DOWN. To raise Pantograph - Press ZPT downwards (i.e. ‘UP’ position) for 1 second. Contactor 130.1 in SB2 closes and rear Pantograph will raise when PSS 85 on Auto position and IG38 key horizontal position in E70 loco and Vertical in KNORR. To lower pantograph- Press ZPT upward (i.e. ‘DOWN’ position) for 1 second, pantograph will lower. Operations of pantograph selector switch (PSS 85) This should be operated when Pantograph in lower condition. PSS 85 has the following three positions. Auto - Always rear pantograph will rise, I - Pantograph above Cab-I end will rise. II - Pantograph above Cab-II end will rise.
!note Note: To isolate pantograph, change PSS 85 to I or II (Electrical isolation) and close the corresponding cut out cock (PAN-1 or PAN-2 Horizontal – open, Vertical – Close) provided on pneumatic panel.

@@3 4.5  Operation of BLDJ
BLDJ switch is used for closing and opening of VCB. VCB can be closed in driving and cooling mode. Spring loaded lever switch BLDJ has 3 positions: ON, 0 and OFF For Closing VCB press BLDJ downward (i.e. ON position) for 1 second VCB closes, LSDJ extinguish. For opening VCB press BLDJ upward (i.e. OFF position) for 1 second VCB opens and LSDJ glows.

@@3 4.6  Operation of Main compressor
The compressors are controlled by RGCP and BLCP. BLCP is a spring-loaded lever switch has 3 positions. BLCP OFF - Both compressors are off. AUTO - Automatic working of MCP controlled by RGCP. MAN - Compressors working continuously till BLCP pressed in MAN position. AUTO position: BLCP in AUTO position and the MR pressure is less than 8 kg/cm 2 , the unloading valve will energise for 4 seconds. BUR will stop and the compressor contactor will close and BUR will start again and frequency will ramp up to 50 Hz within 2 sec. If frequency reaches 50Hz, the unloading valve will close. When pressure builds up to 10 kg/cm 2, frequency of BUR will ramp down from 50Hz to 40 Hz in approximately in 3 sec. MCP contactors opens and BUR again ramp up to 50HZ. If both the compressors are not running for a long time, then the unloading valve will open for 5 sec after every 8 minutes to drain the air-dryer. If the pressure drops below 5.6 kg/cm 2 and MPJ in F/R, the low main reservoir switch closes to initiate S/R inter lock and the message "Low pressure main reservoir" appears on the fault display unit. MAN position: In spring loaded MAN position of BLCP, both compressors run continuously.

@@3 4.7  Operation of Reverser
Reverser has following 3 positions. F – Forward, 0 – Neutral, R – Reverse.
1. Throttle and reverser are interlocked mechanically.
2. Ensure MR pressure is above 6.4 kg/cm 2 .
3. Move the reverser into desired position.
4. Converter contactors close.
5. Direction is set to required position.
6. Loco will configure to driving / shunting mode according to 160 switch position.
7. DC link capacitors are charged.
8. FLG590 appears on the display screen and loco is ready for movement.
!note Note: Keep MPJ in 0 position during cab changing and before attaching brake pipe / feed pipe to formation or if BP, FP and MR pressure drops on run to avoid loco shutdown with fault message F01 10 P1 “Fatal Error in Main circuit” due to converter contactors opening.

@@3 4.8  Operation of Throttle
The TE/BE throttle controls traction and the regenerative braking effort of the locomotive through an angle transmitter and auxiliary contacts. The TE/BE throttle has the following positions: Traction side:
1. Position 1/3 - 33% tractive effort
2. Position 2/3 - 67% tractive effort
3. Position TE max - 100% tractive effort
4. Position 0 - no tractive / no braking effort. Set TE/BE to 0 and move towards traction side as per requirement. TE is possible up to 100%.
Regenerative braking side
1. Position 1/3 - 33% braking effort
2. Position 2/3 - 67% braking effort
3. Position TE max - 100% braking effort Set TE/BE to 0 and move towards braking side. Regenerative Braking is possible up to 0 KMPH. The % of TE/BE is indicated on the bogie meters provided on panel A.
!note Note:
1. Harmonic filter will come in to service as soon as throttle handle is moved from zero. If the harmonic filter isolated, the TE/BE is restricted to 40 KMPH by CE.
2. Whenever throttle is operated to TE side on shunting mode (160 in 0), a message F11 06 P2 will appear in the screen and loco speed is restricted to 15 kmph. Acknowledge the same.
3. If throttle is not at 0 while closing VCB or if throttle is operated before node FLG590, Information message no. 003 will appear on display screen.
‘Traction may not be available on this loco or on the slave loco’. Bring throttle to 0 and ensure FLG590 and again operate the throttle again. 4.If the throttle is on TE side for a more than specified time on standstill condition with / without brake application or speed not achieved as per demanded TE, a fault message “demanded speed cannot be achieved will appear in the screen. DDS reads as Warning Loco creeping”. Troubleshoot as per TSD.

@@3 4.9  Operation of 154 for isolating Bogie
If the loco is running
1. Bring the throttle to 0.
2. Open the VCB.
3. Place 154 switch in required position.
4. Select I for isolating bogie - 1 and II for isolating bogie - 2. (Bogie will be isolated after 10 seconds)
5. LSFI glows continuously, isolation message will appear.
6. Press ‘Enter’ button, isolation message will disappear. If the loco is standstill
1. Bring the throttle to 0.
2. Reverser -F and ensure node FLG590.
3. Place 154 switch in required position.
4. Select I for isolating bogie - 1 and II for isolating bogie – 2(Bogie will be isolated after 10 seconds)
5. LSFI glows continuously, isolation message will appear.
6. Press ‘Enter’ button, isolation message will disappear.
!note Note: If 154 switch placed in wrong position, it may lead to isolation of working bogie and fatal error may experience. During this time, stop train, switch off CE and place 154 switch in correct position. In modified 154 switch the stopper lock is removed and it is possible to rotate in both the directions.

@@3 4.10  Operation of 160
To restrict the speed to 15 KMPH during shunting, the switch 160 is provided in SB1 panel. During shunt movement this switch should be placed in 0 position.
Operation to configure 15kmph:
1. Stop the loco and apply SA9, Ensure BC pressure reads 3.5 Kg/cm 2 .
2. Bring MPJ to 0. (Ensure node FLG570).
3. Change 160 switch from 1 to 0 position.
4. Loco speed restricted to 15KMPH, F11 06 P2 will appear in the display screen whenever throttle is moved to TE side
Operation to configure MPS:
1. Stop the Loco and apply SA9, Ensure BC pressure reads 3.5 Kg/cm 2 .
2. Bring MPJ to 0, (Ensure node FLG570).
3. Change 160 switch from 0 to 1 position.
4. Now loco speed configured to MPS of the loco.
!note Note: Loco Pilot to ensure 160 switch in position ‘1’ before staring train to avoid detention.

@@3 4.11  Operation of 237.1
This switch is provided to isolate VCD only during failure/ mal-functioning.
1. Change 237.1 switch from 1 to 0. (Provided in SB 1 panel).
2. Fault message F11 05 P2 will be displayed in the screen. Acknowledge by pressing BPFA, fault message will disappear and recorded in fault memory.
3. Inform the same to section TLC and record it in the log book. Vigilance Restoration
1. Stop the train/Loco and apply SA9.
2. Change 237.1 switch from 0 to 1 position.
3. Fault message F10 03 P1 will be displayed in the screen.
4. BP pressure drops to 0 and then recharges to 5 Kg/cm 2 .
5. Acknowledge the fault message by pressing BPFA, LSFI and BPFA will extinguish.
6. VCD is restored to service.

@@3 4.12  Operation of A9, SA9 & RB
1. Set the TE/BE throttle to the desired position in the range ‘0’ to BE MAX (braking effort).
2. Move A9 handle from "Run" to "Initial Application". If more brake force is required move the handle to the desired position between "Initial Application" and "Full Service".
3. For gradual release of the automatic train brake, move the handle in the release direction between "Full Service" and "Initial Application" and then to position Run.
4. For quick releasing of automatic train brake, keep the A9 in ‘Release’ (spring Loaded) position for 1 sec.
5. When the train stops apply SA9.
6. Apply the parking brake if required.
!note Note: The regenerative brake is only effective if brake cylinder pressure is "0". To initial charging of BP move handle of the automatic train brake to position "Release". This position has also to be chosen for a fast release of the train brakes.
Operation PVEF While applying A9 press PVEF. Once PVEF is pressed after destroying BP pressure, the conjunction brakes will releases and remains in released condition till further application of A9 even though PVEF is released.
Emergency Braking The emergency brake is independent of the control electronics. It can be activated in the following ways:
1. Move A9 handle to the position emergency.
2. Operating the ALP emergency brake cock.
3. Control Electronics apply emergency brakes during VCD action.
4. Speed exceeds 10% of MPS of the loco.
5. Operation of Emergency stop button and action of TPWS if provided.

@@3 4.13  Parking Brakes
The parking brake units are provided on wheel no. 2, 6,7and11 in WAG9 and 1, 4, 5 and 8 on WAP5. It should be operated when Loco/train on standstill condition only. To lock PB in applied or release condition press application or release plunger and turn the knob in SV30 provided in the pneumatic panel.
Releasing of parking brake S.no. Steps to be followed by crew Effect
1. During Control Electronics ON Press BPPB(A-panel). Red illumination goes OFF on BPPB and parking brake gauge reads 6 kg/cm 2 . parking brakes released.
2. Press releasing plunger in SV30 (pneumatic panel) Parking brake gauge reads 6kg/cm 2
3. Take safety measures and ensure BC and PB gauge reads 0. Manually turn the release handle slightly clockwise and then pull. Release each brake cylinder individually.
Parking brakes released. Ensure brake blocks are released.
Application of parking brake S.no. Steps to be followed by crew Effect
1. During Control Electronics ON - Press BPPB. Red illumination glows on BPPB and PB gauge reads 0 kg/cm 2 and PB applied.
2. Press application plunger in SV30 Parking brake gauge reads 0 kg/cm 2 and PB applied.
3. When BL key is moved from D to OFF, or If M.R pressure drops below 3.5 Kg/ cm 2
Parking Brake gauge reads 0 kg/cm 2 and PB applied.

@@3 4.14  Operation of Sanders
The automatic anti - spin protection is initiated as soon as the ratio between effective TE/BE and requested TE/BE is less than 0.5, LSP glows, TE/BE difference is reduced by control electronics. Sanding is done automatically on leading wheels and when PSA is pressed.
!note Note: Do not press PSA continuously; 10-12 second pause is required between two operations of PSA. Ensure proper closing of sand box covers to avoid entry of water into the boxes.

@@3 4.15  Operation of Head Light
- Head light is working with 110V DC supply.
- MCB for cab-1 is 310.1/1 and for Cab -2 310.1/2 operation.
- Head Light will work only when BL key either D mode or C mode.
- For switching on head light, BLPR switch is provided in C panel.
- ZPRD switch is for Dim/Bright working of head light.
- Contactor for cab-1 head light is 338/1
- Contactor for cab-2 head light is 338/2
- BLPR-1 to F or BLPR-2 to R- cab-1 head light glow
- BLPR-1 to R or BLPR-2 to F- cab-2 head light glow
!note Note: To change head light bulb, open the front cover, slack head light bulb from fixing beading and remove the wire connection to bulb. Replace bulb with new one. Operation of wipers Wiper switch is provided in ALP side on driving desk, Ensure availability of water in the tank.

@@3 4.16  Switch ON / OFF Machine Room Light
- For switching ON machine room Lights.
- BL key should be kept in D, C or self-hold mode.
- Press Latched push-button (Two ways switch provided at both ends of the machine room) for operation of machine room tube lights.
- If required keep BL on C mode for switching on corridor lights during examination of machine room or resetting of MCB or doing some troubleshooting in machine room. Its controlling MCB is 310.4.

@@3 4.17  Operation of Flasher Light And Market Light
1. Flasher light unit is provided in cab at ALP side. Ensure that switch provided is at ON
2. Flasher light unit is independent of CE.
3. To switch on the flasher, rotate the self-illuminated BPFL provided in the C-panel.
4. In non modified locos flasher light works on BL in C or D position only.
5. Automatic flasher light system will work during ICC on RUN.
6. It is not possible to test the auto flasher in standstill condition.
7. Switch on ZLFW for the white marker lights and ZLFR for red marker lights in the C-panel (both red and white marker lights will not glow simultaneously).
8. Marker light will glow when battery MCB 112.1 in switched OFF condition also. (MCB 112 in BB1 should be in ON condition)
9. Switch on the following switches for Cab/ instrument /Desk lights.
1. ZLC- Switch - Cab light
2. ZLI- Switch - Instrument lighting
3. ZLDD-Switch - Desk illumination

@@3 4.18  Active Functions In Rear Cab
1. Gauges, SPM
2. U meter.
3. Emergency brake cock on ALP side (RS)
4. Cab and desk lighting.
5. Windshield wipers.
6. Fault display screen.
7. Cab ventilation and heating
8. Marker lights.
9. Crew fans,
10. Hand lamp socket.

@@3 4.19  Operation of Cab Air Conditioner
Power supply for AC is 3 phase 415V Capacity 2.2 KW, Cooling capacity 1.5 T AT 50◦ C ambient, summer 23 0 C to 25 0 C and winter -19 0 C to 21 0 C. The 3 phase supply taken from BUR-2 via MCB 64.1 (AC MCB in HB-2 panel), contactor 52.7 and 3 pole double throw switch (HB-2 panel-AC unit MCB) to both control units of cab-1 and cab-2. Only one cab AC will run at a time. Change 3 poles double throw MCB in the HB-2 panel according cab operation. Switch on control unit in respective cab after closing machine room door and cab doors.
!note Note: In case of isolation of any of auxiliary converter the AC unit will not run. AC isolating MCB 64.1 and AC unit MCB to both control units of cab- 1and 2 is in HB-2 panel.

@@3 4.20  Operation of Hotel Load
Hotel load is supplied by a separate secondary winding of main transformer: Voltage: 750 V AC,Frequency: 50Hz, Power: 850kVA When the spring-loaded hotel load supply switch BLHO is on, hotel load contactor closes in HL contactor unit via CE. When it is closed, the yellow coloured hotel load supply indication lamp LSHO glows in the cab and two hotel load converters will come into service. The hotel load is connected to the train via IV coupler sockets at both ends of the loco.
Operation
1. Ensure pantograph is lowered and Hotel Load MCB129.1 (SB2) in OFF condition while detaching/attaching of power/control couplers of HOG. After attaching couplers switch on Hotel Load MCB in SB2 and energize Loco.
2. After passing every neutral section, ensure LSHO glowing.
3. HOG cannot be switched ON in the following condition
- MR pressure low (less than 6.4 kg/cm 2 ). If LP tried to switch ON HOG, a fault message F10 02 P1 will be displayed even Reverser in 0.
- Transformer oil Pressure not OK (F01 02 P2)
- STB1 fails HOG cannot be switched on from both cabs.
- STB2 fails HOG cannot be switched on from cab2.
!note Note.
1. Before loco detaching from train, uncouple HOG couplers.
2. Uncoupling / coupling of HOG couplers will be done by power car staff.
3. Written permission from LP, to be obtained from power car staff for uncoupling/coupling.
4. Train should not be delayed on account of troubleshooting of HOG.
5. Train can be started without HOG.
6. Isolation: Hotel Load MCB129.1 feeding control supply to HOG (SB2).
7. Open Hotel Load MCB129.1 to isolate HOG.
MCB 129.1 in SB-2 Panel
!note Note:
1. During troubleshooting if LP unable to switch OFF CE, remove Hotel load UIC coupler and try to switch OFF CE.
2. During troubleshooting of any loco trouble, first switch OFF Hotel load.
3. Repeated opening and closing of VCB leads to power car troubles.
4. If Hotel load UIC of coach is wrongly connected to MU UIC of loco, “TWO cabs occupied” fault message appears.
5. If smoke/fire in Hotel load convertor, LP to extinguish it manually by using portable Fire extinguisher

@@2 5. Resetting of MCB | ch=5
Resetting of MCB and Operation of Contactor
Some times MCB trips momentarily without any equipment failure Driver should try to reset MCB once. For resetting MCBs in HB1 & HB2 panels, VCB to be opened. For resetting MCBs in SB1 & SB2 panels, CE to be switched off, except the following MCBs (which are all controlled by 126/Control circuit contactor)
a) 48.1 Auxiliary compressor.
b) 127.91/1 or 127.91/2 power supply 24V/48 V.
c) 310.7 Marker lights, 310.1/1 or 310.1/2 lighting front.
d) 310.4 Lighting machine room.
e) 127.7 Pneumatic panel.
f) 127.12 Panto/VCB.
g) 127.15 Vigilance Cut out.
h) 127.3/1 Cab - 1.
i) 127.3/2 Cab - 2.
j) 127.81 Commissioning - 1
k) 127.82 Commissioning - 2
For resetting all other MCBs in SB1 and 2 panels, switch OFF CE, reset MCB & switch ON CE.
1. ABB make MCB
2. Merlin Gerin make MCB
3. Schneider make MCB i. Knob type ii. Plunger type iii. Rotating Knob type
4. L&T make MCB (Supernova)
5. Siemens make MCB.
